Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出并精确刻画了TTA选择中的信息通道边界，提供了区分"弱选择器"与"信息不足通道"的实用方法，对无标签自适应部署的可靠性评估具有理论和实践意义。局限在于理论分析基于有限批次Gaussian模型，真实分布偏移的复杂性可能超出该简化模型，且如何构造"分辨相关歧义"的更丰富证据仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers many ways to update a deployed model without labels, but choosing the wrong update can make a strong source model worse. Recent methods therefore try to predict which adaptation will work from unlabeled test data. We ask a prior question: does the evidence given to the selector contain enough information to determine the best action at all? We show that this is not guaranteed, even with a perfect selector. If an observation channel makes two deployments look the same while their TTA rankings differ, reliable selection is impossible from that channel; richer evidence can restore the decision only when it resolves the relevant ambiguity. We make this boundary exact in a finite-batch Gaussian TTA model, where doing nothing beats mean recentering for small shifts, recentering wins beyond a unique critical shift, and the boundary shrinks as $1/\sqrt n$. Public benchmark studies on CIFAR-100-C and DomainNet-126 show the same failure mode with modern TTA methods: changing only deployment structure can reverse the oracle action while global order-blind evidence remains unchanged. The result is a practical way to separate two failure modes that are usually mixed together: a weak selector versus an information channel that cannot support the desired decision in the first place.

</details>


### [30] [HALDETECT at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Answer-First Contrastive Grounding with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11236)
*Syed Mohaiminul Hoque,Md Sakhawat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HALDETECT 是 ImageEval 2026 幻觉检测赛道(Task 1b)的参赛系统：给定图像与三个文化上均合理的英文陈述，识别唯一视觉上真实成立的一项。方法将任务重构为单一对比决策，先输出答案再给出解释，并用 4-bit QLoRA 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct（冻结视觉编码器），在 1000 条测试集上取得 CI 0.035，八支队伍中排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型常以流畅的语言"幻觉"出图像中并不存在的视觉细节，这严重限制了它们在细粒度视觉理解与需要视觉证据支撑的判读任务中的可靠性。ImageEval 2026 Task 1b 正是针对这一问题的诊断性评测：模型必须从三个文化上同样可信、语言上同样自然的候选陈述中，找出唯一真正被图像证据支持的那一个。难点在于候选之间的差异不是明显的事实冲突，而是细微的视觉属性（颜色、纹理、形状、语境）差异，因此模型无法依赖语言先验或常识推理"蒙对"，必须真正对齐图像证据；论文由此将重点放在如何衡量与抑制这类幻觉性选择上。

Method: 系统设计围绕三项要素展开：(1) 任务重构——不采用逐条打分或生成式自由作答，而是把整个题目表述为一个对比式(contrastive)决策，即在三个候选中直接选出唯一视觉有据者；(2) 输出格式——先生成答案再生成解释(answer-before-explanation)，避免长链推理把结论"漂移"到语言上更顺但视觉上不成立的选项；(3) 推理结构化——把解释组织为颜色/纹理、形状/形态、语境三类视觉线索，引导模型关注可验证的视觉属性而非语义联想。模型侧采用 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 进行 4-bit QLoRA 适配器微调，训练中冻结视觉编码器，仅适配语言侧参数；同时系统对比了纯提示(prompting)基线与不同适配器配置，并以答案选项顺序(answer order)作为对照变量，用 1000 条测试集上的 Contrastive Instability (CI) 作为评价指标。团队还做了发布后的配对分析(retrospective paired analysis)与四种训练规模的重播种实验。

Result: 最佳提交适配器在 1000 条测试集上取得 CI 0.035，八支队伍中排名第三。开发期实验表明：答案选项顺序对结果的影响可能大于模型规模，即顺序偏置是一个不可忽视的混淆因素；参数高效适配(QloRA)优于纯提示(prompting)。对发布金标的后验配对分析确认了 QLoRA 相对最佳提示的增益在统计上成立，但无法确认"devtest 所选适配器"与"最佳测试适配器"之间的小差距。四种训练规模全部重播种(reseeding)后，原先观察到的数据规模增益曲线不再复现，说明该缩放趋势对随机种子不稳定。剩余 35 个错误集中在文化上同样合理的功能、材质与识别类细微区分上；简单的多适配器投票(adapter voting)无助于改善。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提供了一个可复现的幻觉检测系统与一套方法论反思：将细粒度视觉判读建模为对比决策、答案前置配合结构化视觉线索推理，并证明参数高效适配显著优于纯提示。其价值更多在于对评测实践的警示——答案顺序效应可超过模型规模、小规模数据缩放曲线在换种子后不成立、亚组间微小差距缺乏统计支撑，提示多模态评测需要控制顺序偏置并报告种子方差。局限在于：方法本身较为直接（QLoRA 微调 + 提示工程），未引入新的架构或视觉表示学习；仅在单一 1000 条测试集与八个队伍的小规模赛道上验证，泛化性证据有限；残余错误集中于文化与材质层面的细粒度区分，说明当前方法尚未真正解决视觉证据与文化常识纠缠时的判读难题。

Abstract: Large multimodal models tend to hallucinate visual detail fluently, which limits their deployment for fine-grained interpretation. We present HALDETECT, our system for the English hallucination-detection track (Task 1b) of ImageEval 2026, in which a system must identify, from an image and three culturally plausible statements, the single visually grounded one. We frame the item as one contrastive decision, emit the answer before its explanation, and structure reasoning around colour/texture, shape/form, and context. Our best submitted adapter fine-tunes Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with 4-bit QLoRA while freezing the vision encoder and reaches Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035 on the 1,000-item test set; we placed third of eight teams. Development experiments show that answer order can matter more than model scale and that adaptation beats prompting alone. Retrospective paired analysis of the released gold labels confirms the QLoRA gain over the best prompt but not the small gap between the devtest-selected and best-test adapters, and reseeding all four training sizes shows that the apparent data-scaling curve does not survive a seed change. The 35 residual errors are culturally plausible function, material, and recognition distinctions; naive adapter voting does not help.

</details>


### [31] [SCINTILLA-SNN: A Spiking Multi-Scale Selective Aggregation Network for Perineural Invasion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11237)
*Youngung Han,Yului Jeong,Kyeonghun Kim,Dohyun Kweon,Suah Park,Hyunsu Go,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Jinyong Jun,Yunho Choe,Yunjin Seo,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SCINTILLA-SNN，一种用于胆管癌（CCA）术前神经周围侵犯（PNI）预测的 3D 脉冲神经网络：四阶段分层骨干 + 多尺度脉冲聚合（MSSA）模块，通过"诊断 token 分数"选择性聚合稀疏、局部化的 PNI 证据，在 182 例 10 年回顾队列上 5 折交叉验证 AUROC 达 0.748，同时相对同结构稠密 MAC 计算降低约 23.18× 推理能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI 是胆管癌重要的预后与治疗决策依据，但术前无创预测困难：MRI 上的 PNI 相关线索微弱（subtle）、稀疏（sparse）、且空间上局限于肿瘤边界附近。标准 3D CNN 与 Transformer 对体数据进行稠密或空间均匀处理，一方面会把微弱的 PNI 证据在全局特征中"稀释"，另一方面在 3D 特征网格上产生大量乘累加（MAC）运算，计算与能耗代价高。因此需要一个既能感知稀疏局部证据、又具备能效优势的 3D 体数据建模方案。

Method: 核心是 SCINTILLA-SNN：由四阶段（four-stage）分层骨干与多尺度脉冲聚合模块（MSSA）组成。骨干通过脉冲卷积阶段（spiking convolutional stages）与局部脉冲窗口调制阶段（local spike window modulation）逐级提取体数据层次化表示。MSSA 在阶段级表示上为每个空间 token 学习一个内容值（content value），并用由放电率（firing rate）与逐时间步膜电位变异性（timestep-wise membrane-potential variability）导出的脉冲动力学门（spike-dynamics gate）对其进行调制，得到"诊断 token 分数"（diagnostic token score），据此对稀疏 PNI 相关证据进行选择性聚合——本质上是把脉冲动力学信号作为稀疏证据的显著性权重，而非均匀处理所有体素/token。整体以脉冲（事件驱动、累积型）计算替代稠密 MAC。

Result: 在 10 年回顾性队列、182 例 CCA 患者数据上评估，采用 5 折交叉验证，评价指标为 AUROC。SCINTILLA-SNN 取得 AUROC 0.748；能耗方面，相比同一网络结构的稠密 MAC-only 计算，估计推理能耗降低 23.18×。摘要未给出与 3D CNN/Transformer 基线的具体数值对比、消融实验细节或统计显著性检验。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 3D 脉冲神经网络引入 CCA 术前 PNI 预测这一临床任务，并以 MSSA 模块（结合放电率与膜电位变异性的脉冲动力学门 + 诊断 token 分数）实现稀疏、边界局部化证据的选择性聚合，同时获得显著能效收益。意义在于为稀疏/微弱医学影像线索建模提供了一种事件驱动、低能耗的体数据范式，并以回顾性队列初步验证了可行性。局限：样本量较小（182 例）且为单中心回顾性研究，AUROC 0.748 绝对性能有限；缺乏与稠密 3D CNN/Transformer 基线的直接性能对比与消融分析；能耗为"估计值"而非实测硬件功耗；脉冲网络在 MRI 体数据上的时间步设置与推理延迟未报告。与事件相机/脉冲神经形态计算的关联体现在其 SNN 脉冲动力学建模与能效优势，但本文任务为静态 MRI 体数据，不涉及真实事件流或 UAV/机器人实时感知场景。

Abstract: Preoperative prediction of perineural invasion (PNI) in cholangiocarcinoma (CCA) is clinically valuable but remains challenging because PNI-related cues on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) are subtle, sparse, and spatially localized around the tumor boundary. Standard 3D CNN and transformer architectures process volumetric data in a dense or spatially uniform manner, which can dilute subtle PNI-related evidence while requiring a large number of multiply-accumulate operations over 3D feature grids. To address these limitations, we propose SCINTILLA-SNN, a 3D spiking network composed of a four-stage hierarchical backbone and a Multi-Scale Spike Aggregation (MSSA) module for PNI prediction. The backbone extracts hierarchical volumetric representations through spiking convolutional stages and local spike window modulation stages. Given the resulting stage-wise representations, MSSA maps each spatial token to a learnable content value and modulates it with a spike-dynamics gate derived from firing rate and timestep-wise membrane-potential variability. The resulting score, referred to as the diagnostic token score, is used to selectively aggregate sparse PNI-related evidence. Experiments on a 10-year retrospective cohort of 182 CCA patients show that SCINTILLA-SNN achieves an AUROC of 0.748 under 5-fold cross-validation, while reducing the estimated inference energy by 23.18$\times$ compared with dense MAC-only computation of the same network.

</details>


### [32] [Fast and Accurate Monomodal 3D High Resolution Deep Registration of Drosophila Larval Brain Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11240)
*Daniel Reisenbüchler,Yousef Sadegheih,Michael Dittrich,Pratibha Kumari,Muhammad Usman,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个训练式的深度可变形配准流水线，在单次前向传播中将果蝇幼虫大脑高分辨率地配准到共享解剖参考模板，速度快于经典方法1–2个数量级，并在提出留出集上取得最高精度，同时开源了网络权重与完整流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇幼虫是神经科学中的紧凑模式系统，其遗传工具可在特定神经群体中表达荧光标记；要跨个体比较这些表达模式，必须先把每个大脑配准到统一的解剖参考空间。现有流水线以经典配准方法为主，对每个体数据都要重新优化、常需逐例调参，且每个大脑耗时以分钟计，难以作为常规预处理步骤。作者因此希望用学习式深度配准替代这一流程，兼顾精度、速度以及对采集质量下降的鲁棒性。

Method: 核心是一个训练好的深度可变形配准网络：给定一个幼虫大脑体数据，网络通过单次前向传播直接输出把该大脑形变对齐到参考模板的变换，并在高空间分辨率下工作。其处理体数据的体素数远超一般学习式3D配准通常报道的规模。论文还配套提出为使该方法可实际应用所需的预处理流程，以及"解剖锚定"（anatomy-anchored）的评价流水线，用于在解剖学意义上评估配准质量。此外发布网络结构、训练权重与全流程的开源框架。

Result: 在一个采用不同采集条件与质量分层的留出集（held-out collection）上，与11个经典配准基线和7个其他学习式基线比较：该方法精度最高，在解剖标志点局部互信息（landmark-local mutual information）指标上比最强的经典基线高出23个百分点；配准速度比经典可变形流水线快1–2个数量级；并且随着采集质量下降，其精度保持率优于所有其他方法。评测指标聚焦于解剖标志点处的局部互信息。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是首次（在幼虫大脑这一大体积、高分辨率场景下）用单次前向传播的深度可变形配准取代逐例优化的经典流程，同时提供了可复现的预处理与解剖锚定的评价体系，并开源网络与权重，使幼虫大脑配准可作为常规预处理步骤。意义在于降低跨个体神经解剖比较的算力与人工调参成本，并提升低质量数据下的可用性。局限方面，摘要未说明其泛化到其他物种/成像模态的程度，解剖锚定评价依赖标志点标注，且训练依赖成对的参考模板对齐数据。

Abstract: The larval stage of Drosophila melanogaster is a compact model system for neuroscience whose genetic toolkit allows fluorescent markers to be expressed in defined neural populations, and comparing the resulting expression patterns across animals requires every brain to be registered into a shared anatomical reference space. Existing pipelines for this task are predominantly based on classical registration methods, which perform a new optimization for each volume, often require per-case parameter tuning, and can take minutes per brain, limiting their use as a routine preprocessing step. We present a trained deep registration pipeline that deformably aligns a larval brain to a reference template in a single forward pass at high spatial resolution, on volumes that hold several times more voxels than those learned 3D registration is normally reported on, together with the preprocessing and anatomy-anchored evaluation pipeline required to apply it. Against eleven classical and seven further learned baselines on a held-out collection acquired with different acquisition and quality strata, the proposed pipeline is the most accurate, improving on the strongest classical baseline by 23 percentage points of anatomical landmark-local mutual information. It registers a volume one to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical deformable pipelines, and it retains more of its accuracy than any other method as acquisition quality degrades. The network, its trained weights and the full pipeline are released as the open-source deep larval brain registration framework: https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg

</details>


### [33] [From Evaluation to Enhancement: Benchmarking and Improving Think-with-Video Reasoning for Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11242)
*Meng Luo,Yicheng Liu,Jiahao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VWG-Bench 基准与 Vid-PRE 提示改写模型，系统性评估并提升视频生成模型的"视频思考"能力（符号推理、物理规律、目标导向），揭示当前模型渲染质量与认知正确性之间的显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成虽能产出视觉逼真、时序连贯的结果，但是否真正具备"用视频思考"的能力——执行符号规则、遵守物理定律、追求意图目标——仍悬而未决。既有基准多将视觉质量与认知正确性混为一谈，缺乏对推理能力的细粒度诊断，导致无法定位模型究竟在哪些规则约束或逻辑推理环节失败。该问题对于评估视频生成作为世界模型（world model）的可信度与实用性至关重要。

Method: 工作分为两部分：(1) VWG-Bench——覆盖 9 个推理维度、38 个细粒度任务的综合性基准；并设计三级 VLM-as-Judge 评估协议，分别独立考察视频级流畅度（fluency）、任务级规则遵循度（rule adherence）与样本级目标实现度（goal realization），实现精准诊断。(2) Vid-PRE（Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer）——一个模型无关（model-agnostic）的提示重写器，将认知推理负担从生成器卸载至专用 VLM；通过强化学习训练，且仅使用纯文本奖励（text-based rewards），避免视频级奖励信号的不稳定性，输出简洁且约束感知的提示。

Result: 对多个领先视频生成模型的评测显示：模型在渲染分数上表现强劲，但在逻辑密集与规则约束任务上持续失败，凸显渲染质量与认知正确性之间的"显著鸿沟"。引入 Vid-PRE 后，在多个生成器上均带来显著的推理性能提升，且无需任何架构修改，验证了提示层面推理卸载的有效性与可扩展性。数据与代码已在 HuggingFace 公开（KlingTeam/VWG-Bench）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提供了细粒度、可诊断的"视频思考"评测基准 VWG-Bench，以及一条模型无关、训练稳定的推理增强路径 Vid-PRE，为迈向真正的 think-with-video 能力提供了严格诊断视角与可扩展方案。潜在局限在于：Vid-PRE 依赖外部 VLM 与文本奖励，其推理能力上限受限于所选用 VLM，且纯文本训练是否能完全泛化到所有视觉-物理推理场景仍待验证。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced to produce visually compelling and temporally coherent results. Yet, whether these models can genuinely think with video--executing symbolic rules, respecting physical laws, and pursuing intentional goals--remains an open question. Existing benchmarks only partially address this, often conflating visual quality with cognitive correctness. We introduce VWG-Bench (Video World Generalist Benchmark), a comprehensive benchmark spanning 9 reasoning dimensions and 38 fine-grained tasks. To enable precise diagnosis, we design a three-level VLM-as-Judge protocol that independently assesses video-level fluency, task-level rule adherence, and sample-level goal realization. Evaluations of leading models reveal a striking gap: while models achieve strong rendering scores, they consistently fail on logic-heavy and rule-constrained tasks. To address this, we propose Vid-PRE (Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer), a model-agnostic prompt rewriter that offloads the cognitive burden of reasoning to a dedicated VLM. Trained via reinforcement learning with purely text-based rewards, Vid-PRE produces concise, constraint-aware prompts without the instability of video-level reward signals. Experiments show that Vid-PRE yields substantial reasoning improvements across multiple generators without architectural modifications. Together, VWG-Bench and Vid-PRE offer a rigorous diagnostic lens and a scalable path toward true think-with-video capabilities. All data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KlingTeam/VWG-Bench.

</details>


### [34] [Uncertainty DMD: Restoring Diversity in Few-Step Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11265)
*Zixuan Duan,Xunzhi Xiang,Yabo Chen,Xin Zhang,Changhan Liu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对少步蒸馏（few-step distillation）在自回归（AR）视频生成中导致的多样性崩溃问题，提出 Uncertainty DMD。作者发现 DMD 蒸馏后的 AR 视频生成器存在"结构化不确定性崩溃"：DMD 的 mode-seeking 偏向把不同噪声样本映射为几乎相同的第一段（first chunk），随后确定性的 AR cache 将该坍缩状态传播至后续所有 chunk，使局部的随机性丢失演变为全局的时序变化抑制。Uncertainty DMD 通过在两个关键阶段注入不确定性（首 chunk 的时间步扰动 + 后续 chunk 的随机 cache 写入），无需改动架构即可恢复随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏能显著提升自回归视频生成的推理效率，但常引发多样性崩溃：同一 prompt 下不同噪声样本生成高度相似的视频，运动动态被削弱。作者指出该问题在 AR 视频生成中并非普通的多样性下降，而是具有结构性的成因——膨胀的确定性（deterministic AR cache）会把第一段的状态坍缩放大为整个时间序列的全局确定性，因此需要在 AR rollout 的根节点与传播路径上分别处理。理解并解决该问题对于在保持高效少步采样的同时保留视频的丰富运动与样本多样性具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先对 Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）蒸馏的 AR 视频生成器进行退化机理分析，提出"结构化不确定性崩溃"：DMD 的 mode-seeking 偏差使不同噪声样本被映射到近乎一致的第一段，确定性的 AR cache 再把坍缩状态传播到后续所有 chunk。基于此分析提出 Uncertainty DMD，一个无需架构修改、仅引入轻量扰动操作的不确定性注入框架，在 AR 生成的两个阶段恢复随机性：(1) 首 chunk 的时间步扰动（timestep perturbation），以提升第一段的多样性；(2) 后续 chunk 的随机 cache 写入机制（stochastic cache-writing），在自回归条件中保留不确定性。同一套扰动机制在训练与推理阶段均被使用。

Result: 论文通过实验评估 Uncertainty DMD 在少步 DMD 蒸馏 AR 视频生成器上的表现（具体数据集与评测指标需参见正文）。结果显示：该方法在保持每样本视觉质量相当的前提下，持续改善生成多样性（diversity）与运动动态（motion dynamics），证明局部随机性的恢复可有效缓解全局时序变化的抑制。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 揭示并形式化解释了 DMD 蒸馏 AR 视频生成中"结构化不确定性崩溃"的退化机理，指出确定性 AR cache 是局部坍缩向全局传播的关键；(2) 提出 Uncertainty DMD，通过首 chunk 时间步扰动与随机 cache 写入两处轻量不确定性注入，在不改变架构的前提下恢复随机性，并统一用于训练与推理。意义在于为高效少步 AR 视频生成中多样性—效率的权衡提供了新的分析视角与简单有效的解决方案。局限（基于摘要）：具体实验规模、数据集与量化指标细节需见正文；perturbation 强度等超参的敏感性及对其他蒸馏范式的泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Few-step distillation improves the efficiency of autoregressive (AR) video generation, but often causes diversity collapse: under the same prompt, different noise samples tend to produce highly similar videos with weakened motion dynamics. We analyze this degradation in Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-distilled AR video generators and find that, in the autoregressive setting, it takes the form of a structured uncertainty collapse: the mode-seeking bias of DMD maps different noise samples to nearly identical first chunks, and the deterministic AR cache then propagates this collapsed state to all subsequent chunks, turning a local loss of stochasticity at the rollout root into a global suppression of temporal variation. Based on this analysis, we propose Uncertainty DMD, a simple uncertainty-injection framework that restores stochasticity at two key stages of AR generation: a timestep perturbation for the first chunk to increase first-chunk diversity, and a stochastic cache-writing mechanism for later chunks to preserve uncertainty in autoregressive conditioning. The method requires no architectural changes and introduces only lightweight perturbation operations. The same perturbation mechanisms are used during both training and inference. Experiments show that Uncertainty DMD consistently improves diversity and motion dynamics while maintaining comparable per-sample visual quality.

</details>


### [35] [Improving Faint Object Detection for Space Situational Awareness with Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11269)
*Angela Cratere,Luca Ghilardi,Vishnu Reddy,Francesco Dell'Olio,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Roberto Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个深度学习流水线，通过自动化恒星去除与背景重建，增强光学空间态势感知（SSA）中暗弱运动目标的检测能力；核心为 Tiny-U-Net 星像分割 + astro-VAE 背景修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学空间态势感知（SSA）中，尤其是地月空间（X-GEO）区域，低信噪比（SNR）目标检测极具挑战：结构化天空背景、密集星场与散射月光会严重削弱经典检测算法的性能。传统方法难以在抑制固定源（恒星）与背景不均匀性的同时保留暗弱运动目标，因此需要一种数据驱动的预处理策略来提升目标可检测性。

Method: 方法为两阶段深度学习流水线：(1) 使用轻量级分割网络 Tiny-U-Net 生成恒星掩膜（stellar mask），实现固定源定位与剔除；(2) 采用部分卷积变分自编码器 astro-VAE，学习天文背景的统计分布，对掩膜区域进行上下文感知（context-aware）修复/inpainting，从而重建无星背景图。重建背景图作为预处理步骤，抑制固定源与背景不均匀性，随后接入 shift-and-stack（移位叠加）方案进行目标检测。部分卷积（partial convolution）用于确保修复仅依赖有效像素，避免掩膜污染。

Result: 作为概念验证，方法在真实地基望远镜观测、针对 X-GEO 区域的图像数据上进行评估，并与 shift-and-stack 方案集成。结果显示：方法能够高保真地重建无星背景，同时在去除恒星的过程中保留运动目标，显著提升了目标可检测性（detectability）。摘要未提供定量指标（如 SNR 增益、ROC/AUC、具体数量指标），仅给出定性/保真度层面的结论。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种面向光学 SSA 暗弱运动目标检测的数据驱动预处理策略，通过深度学习实现恒星去除与背景重建，并在真实 X-GEO 观测上验证了可行性。贡献在于将分割与生成式修复结合用于天文背景建模，作为下游检测流水线的前置模块。潜在局限：仅作为概念验证（proof of concept），缺少定量评测指标与消融实验；对密集星场、散月光等极端条件的鲁棒性、泛化到不同望远镜/天区的能力，以及 astro-VAE 重建误差对后续检测的影响，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present a deep-learning pipeline for enhancing the detection of faint moving objects in optical space situational awareness (SSA) imagery through automated star removal and background reconstruction. Detecting low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) objects remains extremely challenging in optical observations, particularly in the cislunar (X-GEO) environment, where structured sky backgrounds, dense stellar fields, and scattered moonlight significantly degrade the performance of classical detection algorithms. To address this problem, the proposed pipeline combines a lightweight segmentation network (Tiny-U-Net) to generate stellar masks with a partial-convolution variational autoencoder (astro-VAE), designed to learn the statistical distribution of astronomical backgrounds and perform context-aware inpainting of masked regions. The reconstructed background maps can then be used as a preprocessing step to suppress fixed sources and background inhomogeneities prior to detection. As a proof of concept, the approach is integrated with a shift-and-stack scheme and evaluated on real ground-based telescope observations targeting the X-GEO region. Results demonstrate that the method reconstructs star-free backgrounds with high fidelity, while preserving moving targets and significantly enhancing detectability, thereby providing an effective data-driven preprocessing strategy for faint moving-object detection in optical SSA scenarios.

</details>


### [36] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Order-Aware Slab MIL（OAS-MIL），一种用于术前 T2 加权 MRI 肝内胆管癌（ICC）患者级神经周围侵犯（PNI）预测的弱监督框架。将肿瘤 MRI 裁剪块建模为沿轴位方向排列的重叠 2.5D slab 序列，结合置换不变集合注意力与双向序列注意力聚合，五折交叉验证平均 AUROC 达 0.770，优于体素级与常规 MIL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI 是 ICC 的不良预后指标，但通常只能通过术后切除标本病理确认，无法在术前指导治疗决策。术前 T2 加权 MRI 可能携带可预测 PNI 的非侵入性影像线索，但监督信号仅存在于患者层面，缺少切片级或体素级标注，因此该问题本质上是一个患者级多示例学习（MIL）问题。如何在仅有弱标签的条件下有效利用 MRI 的空间与轴向结构信息，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 1）数据表示：以肿瘤为中心裁剪 MRI，将连续轴位切片组合为相互重叠的 2.5D slab，每个病例被表示为有序的 slab 序列，从而在保留轴向层间上下文的同时避免三维卷积的显存与样本量瓶颈。2）编码：使用共享编码器提取每个 slab 的级特征（slab-level features）。3）聚合：设计双分支聚合模块——置换不变集合注意力分支（permutation-invariant set-attention）用于建模 slab 间的无序显著示例，以及双向序列注意力分支（bidirectional sequence-attention）用于显式捕捉轴位方向的顺序结构。4）融合两个分支的表示进行患者级 PNI 二分类，实现端到端弱监督训练，其关键归纳偏置是"轴向顺序"信息。

Result: 实验采用患者级、按标签分层的五折交叉验证。主要评价指标为 AUROC，OAS-MIL 取得平均 AUROC 0.770，优于所评估的 volumetric 与常规 MIL 基线，表明引入轴向顺序建模相对于纯集合式聚合带来性能提升。摘要未报告额外数据集、切片规模、显著性检验或 AUC 之外的指标（如准确率、F1、校准）等细节。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：（1）提出面向患者级弱监督 PNI 预测的 OAS-MIL 框架；（2）通过有序重叠 2.5D slab 表示与集合注意力＋双向序列注意力的双分支聚合，将轴向顺序作为有效归纳偏置引入 MIL；（3）在 ICC 术前 T2 加权 MRI 上验证其优于 volumetric 与 MIL 基线。意义在于为无法获得体素级标注的术前影像标志物预测提供了一条可行路径。潜在局限包括：AUROC 0.770 仍属中等水平，临床可用性尚需外部多中心验证；仅使用 T2 加权单一模态；患者级标签无法提供病灶定位可解释性；摘要未说明 slab 数量、重叠比例、编码器骨干及计算开销等实现细节。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>


### [37] [SAMV-DUSt3R: Instance-Centric 3D Scene Decoupling from Sparse Multi-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11279)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Yingdan Zhang,Pengfei Zhao,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMV-DUSt3R 是一个端到端模型，将 SAM2 的 2D 实例分割掩码注入 MV-DUSt3R 多视图重建流程中，实现单阶段的对象级 3D 场景解耦与重建；其中 Cross Flow Mask Block 利用掩码引导网络聚焦目标实例，Spatial RankGNN 则用于选择最优参考视图以保证重建稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 3D 场景中解耦出独立对象是驱动、机器人、AR/VR、文化遗产数字化等应用的核心需求。然而，现有的对象级 3D 重建方法通常依赖多阶段流水线（先分割、再重建、后融合），流程复杂且容易产生误差累积。作者希望构建一个端到端框架，在重建过程中引入 2D 语义/实例先验，同时提升形状精度并实现对象级别的场景解耦，避免多阶段方案带来的不一致性。

Method: 该方法以 MV-DUSt3R（多视图 3D 重建模型）为基础，将 SAM2 生成的 2D 实例掩码注入重建网络。核心组件包括：（1）Cross Flow Mask Block——利用 2D 掩码作为引导信号，使网络流（flow）聚焦到目标实例上，从而在单阶段内联合优化形状精度与对象级解耦；（2）轻量级 Spatial RankGNN——对候选参考视图进行排序，自动选择最优参考视图，以保证重建的稳定性。整体为端到端架构，无需多阶段管线。

Result: 论文在多种评估指标下进行了广泛实验，结果显示相比当前最先进（SOTA）基线，平均重建精度提升约 11%。此外，Spatial RankGNN 的参考视图选择准确率达到 73.5%。实验结果表明该方法具有较强的实例解耦能力和稳定的重建能力，但其具体数据集名称、评价度量细节与消融实验在摘要中未明确给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一种端到端、单阶段的实例级 3D 重建框架，通过 Cross Flow Mask Block 将 SAM2 掩码与 MV-DUSt3R 深度融合，实现形状精度与对象解耦的联合提升，并用 Spatial RankGNN 提升参考视图选择的稳定性，在多个指标上取得约 11% 的平均精度增益。其意义在于为驱动、机器人、AR/VR 和遗产数字化等场景提供了更简洁、更准确的实例解耦方案。潜在局限包括：论文未披露具体数据集与评测协议，73.5% 的视图选择准确率仍有提升空间，方法性能可能受限于 SAM2 掩码质量，且摘要中未讨论计算开销与实时性。

Abstract: With the rising demand to decouple objects from 3D scenes, we propose SAMV-DUSt3R, an end-to-end model that injects SAM2 2D masks into MV-DUSt3R reconstruction. A Cross Flow Mask Block uses these masks to steer the network toward the target instance, jointly improving shape accuracy and achieving object-level disentanglement without multi-stage pipelines. To ensure reconstruction stability, a lightweight Spatial RankGNN selects the optimal reference view with a selection accuracy of 73.5\%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method boosts average reconstruction precision by 11\% across various metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results reveal a strong instance-disentanglement capability and clear benefits for driving, robotics, AR/VR, and heritage digitisation.

</details>


### [38] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视软提示（soft prompting）作为视觉-语言模型（VLM）少样本域外目标检测的适配方案：在冻结预训练主干的前提下，仅优化少量连续提示token（平均约7,168个参数），即可在Roboflow20-VL（10-shot）上达到14.2 mAP，与最佳LoRA配置持平，但训练参数量少20,000倍以上，且不会造成预训练能力的灾难性遗忘。作者强调两个关键设计：提示token应置于视觉与文本token之间的跨模态边界处，并以空空间token（empty space token）进行初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对的是域外（out-of-domain）场景（如航拍、工业、医疗影像）下的少样本目标检测问题，监督信号仅有10张标注图像。现有VLM适配手段主要是离散提示优化和LoRA微调。LoRA虽有效，但会引入参数更新并带来对预训练知识的遗忘风险（在NaturalBench VQA上相对下降35%–56%），而离散提示优化表达能力有限。作者希望探究第三条路径——软提示是否能以极低参数代价同时保持性能与预训练能力，从而验证"现代VLM是否已经编码了专业领域所需的大部分知识，真正的挑战只是学会如何提问"这一假设。该问题对无人机、机器人等需要低成本快速适配到新领域且不能牺牲通用能力的实时感知系统尤为重要。

Method: 核心方法是软提示：冻结预训练VLM主干，仅优化少量（1–3个）连续提示token。论文识别并验证了两个关键设计选择：(1) 提示token的放置位置——将其置于视觉token与文本token之间的跨模态边界（cross-modal boundary）处，优于其他放置方式（10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP）；(2) 初始化策略——由空空间token（empty space token）初始化优于语义初始化与随机初始化。学习到的token表现为"提示"而非"权重"：它们可迁移到更新的模型（Qwen3.5-9B）而无需重训练（+0.8 mAP），并可被语言化（verbalize）为可读提示，效果与提示搜索方法相当（匹配DetPO、超过GEPA）。方法还扩展到检测之外：在RoboCasa操作任务上，对冻结的π₀.₅视觉-语言-动作策略应用软提示，当token置于梯度瓶颈（gradient bottleneck）处时，在三个任务中的两个上与LoRA基线持平。

Result: 实验主要在Roboflow20-VL数据集上以10-shot设置评测，指标为mAP。1–3个学习token（平均7,168参数）达到14.2 mAP，匹配最佳LoRA配置，但训练参数少逾20,000倍。软提示优化难度更高，跨随机种子的方差较大。关键对比在于遗忘：与本文精度相当的LoRA秩会使NaturalBench VQA精度相对下降35%，最大秩时达56%，而软提示保持预训练性能不变。迁移性方面，学习token可零重训练迁移到Qwen3.5-9B（+0.8 mAP）。可解释性方面，token可语言化为可读提示，匹配DetPO、优于GEPA。跨任务方面，在RoboCasa操作任务上，冻结的π₀.₅策略经软提示后在三个任务中的两个上匹配LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是系统性地确立软提示作为VLM少样本域外适配的高效替代方案，明确了跨模态边界放置与空空间token初始化两个关键设计，并揭示其在参数效率、零遗忘、跨模型迁移与可语言化方面的优势，同时验证了向视觉-语言-动作策略的泛化性。其意义在于支持"现代VLM已蕴含专业领域所需知识，关键在于学会如何提问"的观点，对航拍/无人机、工业与医疗等低标注、需保留通用能力的感知场景具有价值。局限包括：软提示优化难度大、跨种子方差高，训练稳定性不如LoRA；实验中mAP绝对值不高，且未在更多域外数据集上充分验证；可语言化提示仅达到与提示搜索方法相当而非超越的水平。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [39] [GRIPNet: Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Guided Architecture for Pulmonary Nodule Detection in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11312)
*Haojie Yang,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示肺结节强度呈几何中心峰值、径向高斯衰减的规律，提出将检测器每个模块对应到强度分布可测属性的GRIPNet，在多个公开数据集上显著提升小肺结节检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌致死率居各类恶性肿瘤之首，低剂量CT筛查是早期诊断的主要途径，其成败关键在于最小病灶的检出。然而小于6毫米的肺结节仍然难以检测，原因在于多数方法把结节当作通用目标处理，忽略了决定其外观的成像物理规律。方形卷积在两个轴上均匀采样，与结节的径向信号不匹配，对小尺寸结节的失配尤为严重，因此需要一种与该径向先验对齐的检测架构。

Method: 作者首先验证外观规律：对来自三个公开基准的18,218个标注病灶进行径向高斯拟合，每个数据集、每个尺寸分层的平均径向决定系数均高于0.86，表明结节强度以几何中心为峰值并按高斯模式径向衰减。据此提出GRIPNet（Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Network），使全部模块对应到强度分布的可测量属性：1）Pinwheel convolutions（风车卷积）分解径向梯度；2）dual-frequency模块分离边界细节与结构上下文；3）dilated masked attention匹配衰减范围；4）adaptive loss依据显著度对样本重加权。整体设计把成像物理先验直接编码进检测器的算子结构中。

Result: 在KanserSet、LUNA16和Lung-PET-CT-Dx三个数据集上评估，GRIPNet将mAP@0.5分别提升至95.3%、91.6%和97.9%，同时在实时速度下改善了高IoU定位精度。实验覆盖不同数据集与尺寸分层，验证了径向高斯先验建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于：量化证明肺结节强度呈规则的径向高斯衰减，并据此构建模块级的先验对齐检测器GRIPNet，在小结节检测与高IoU定位上取得显著提升且保持实时性。其意义在于把"结节作为通用目标"的范式转向"成像物理驱动的检测器设计"。潜在局限包括：高斯先验依赖标注病灶的统计拟合，可能对非规则、钙化或贴近胸膜/血管的结节适用性有限；抽象未说明实时速度的具体指标、硬件平台，也未报告与既有方法的完整对比与跨数据集泛化细节。

Abstract: Lung cancer causes more deaths than any other malignancy, and low-dose CT screening is the main pathway to early diagnosis. That pathway hinges on the smallest lesions, yet nodules below six millimeters remain hard to detect, because most methods treat a nodule as a generic object and ignore the imaging physics behind its appearance. We show that this appearance is highly regular. Intensity peaks at the geometric center of a nodule and decays radially in a Gaussian pattern, and a fit to 18,218 annotated lesions from three public benchmarks yields a mean radial coefficient of determination above 0.86 in every dataset and size stratum. A square convolution samples both axes uniformly and is mismatched to this radial signal, most severely for small nodules. Guided by this evidence, we propose GRIPNet (Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Network), a detector in which every module maps to a measurable property of the intensity distribution. Pinwheel convolutions decompose radial gradients, a dual-frequency module separates boundary detail from structural context, dilated masked attention matches the decay extent, and an adaptive loss reweights samples by conspicuity. GRIPNet raises mAP@0.5 to 95.3, 91.6 and 97.9 percent on KanserSet, LUNA16 and Lung-PET-CT-Dx while sharpening high-IoU localization at real-time speed.

</details>


### [40] [Mi-Ripple: Restoring Images Degraded by Iterative AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11317)
*Jiayin Chen,Yicheng Xu,Muting Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mi-Ripple 是一种诊断引导的图像修复工作流，用于抑制迭代参考条件图像编辑中出现的"数字波纹"（栅格状与颗粒状伪影），同时保护图像结构。方法将周期性晶格伪影与内容纠缠的颗粒纹理分离，结合选择性频谱陷波、结构感知平滑与干净参考再生三级手段，在低失真前提下实现伪影抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式的参考条件图像编辑（reference-conditioned image editing）在反复使用参考图像进行生成/编辑时会引入"数字波纹"（digital ripple）——表现为栅格状（grid-like）和颗粒状（granular）纹理。这类伪影既有频谱上可分离的周期性成分，也有与图像内容纠缠、难以区分于真实细节的颗粒成分。单纯优化频谱分数（spectral score）往往无法对应到视觉质量的真实改善，甚至可能因过度滤波而抹除合法细节。因此需要一个既能可量化地降低伪影、又能保护图像结构、并与肉眼可见的清晰度提升相挂钩的修复方案。

Method: Mi-Ripple 的核心在于对伪影按性质分类处理，而非统一滤波：1）诊断阶段将伪影解耦为"周期性晶格伪影"（spectrally isolated periodic lattice artifacts）与"内容纠缠的颗粒纹理"（content-entangled granular texture）；2）对频谱上孤立可分离的周期性伪影采用选择性频谱陷波（selective spectral notching），实现低失真滤波；3）对颗粒纹理采用结构感知平滑（structure-aware smoothing）；4）当直接滤波会抹除合法细节时，转而通过干净参考再生（cleaned-reference regeneration）进行视觉重建。这种"先诊断、再分流"的流程使滤波强度与伪影可分离程度相匹配，避免一刀切式的频谱抑制。

Result: 实验以十四次仅陷波（notch-only）执行为评估设置，采用全图残差标准差（whole-image residual standard deviation）作为度量，在 CIELAB 明度（lightness）单位下为 0.08–0.44。在一个配对的再生（paired regeneration）示例中，参考图像清洗使输出碎屑密度（debris density）降低 45%。作者强调该工作将可测量的伪影缩减与视觉上更干净的生成图像联系起来，而非仅优化单一的频谱评价指标。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一套诊断引导的分流式修复流程，把数字波纹拆解为周期性晶格成分与内容纠缠颗粒成分，并分别用选择性频谱陷波、结构感知平滑和干净参考再生加以处理，从而在低失真的前提下抑制伪影并保护结构，同时用残差标准差与碎屑密度等指标把定量改善与视觉改善对应起来。可能的局限在于：评估规模较小（仅十四次 notches-only 执行与单一配对再生示例），缺乏与现有去伪影/去噪方法的系统性定量对比，且残差标准差与碎屑密度等自定指标与人类主观质量评价之间的相关性尚未验证；对强内容纠缠或非周期性伪影的场景，方法的分流判据是否稳健也需进一步检验。

Abstract: Iterative reference-conditioned image editing can introduce grid-like and granular textures, commonly described as digital ripple. We present Mi-Ripple, a diagnosis-guided restoration workflow that suppresses this digital ripple while protecting image structure. Mi-Ripple separates periodic lattice artifacts from content-entangled granular texture, then combines selective spectral notching, structure-aware smoothing, and cleaned-reference regeneration. This separation enables low-distortion filtering when artifacts are spectrally isolated and visual reconstruction when filtering would erase legitimate detail. Across fourteen notch-only executions, whole-image residual standard deviation is 0.08--0.44 in CIELAB lightness units. In a paired regeneration example, reference cleaning reduces output debris density by 45\%. Mi-Ripple links measurable artifact reduction to visibly cleaner generated images, rather than optimizing a spectral score alone.

</details>


### [41] [Predictive Multi-Landmark OCT Tracking for Increased Motion Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11330)
*Konrad Reuter,Suresh Guttikonda,Chaitali Uday Karekar,Christian Betz,Alexander Schlaefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测的光学相干断层扫描（OCT）无标记运动跟踪方法，通过在多个被跟踪地标之间传播位置更新来获得全局位姿预测，从而在高速度下实现更鲁棒的6D跟踪，在最高100 mm/s速度和最多九个连续跟踪地标下RMS误差低于1 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT因高空间分辨率和固有深度感知能力，成为无标记运动跟踪的有前景模态。但现有OCT跟踪方法在可跟踪速度上受限，尤其是在6D位姿估计中需要顺序跟踪多个地标时，逐地标串行跟踪会累积误差并限制高速运动下的鲁棒性。提升高速运动跟踪能力对将OCT应用于动态场景（如手术导航、机器人感知中的精细运动监测）具有重要意义。

Method: 提出一种预测式跟踪框架：不将多个地标视为相互独立的顺序跟踪任务，而是在被跟踪的多个地标之间传播位置更新，进而融合得到全局位姿预测。通过地标间的运动信息传播与预测，缓解单个地标跟踪在高速度下的失效问题，提升整体6D位姿估计的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在速度高达100 mm/s、连续跟踪地标多达九个的条件下，均方根误差（RMSE）保持在1 mm以下，验证了全局运动传播与预测对提升OCT跟踪鲁棒性的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出全局运动传播与预测策略，突破现有OCT无标记跟踪在可跟踪速度和多位姿估计鲁棒性上的限制。结果显示出将预测机制引入多标记OCT跟踪的潜力，有望推动OCT在高速、高精度运动跟踪场景中的应用。局限在于摘要未给出具体数据集、对比基线及更广速度区间的定量结果，其泛化性与实时性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography is a promising modality for markerless motion tracking due to its high spatial resolution and inherent depth perception. However, existing OCT-based tracking approaches are limited in terms of trackable velocity, particularly when multiple landmarks are tracked sequentially for 6D pose estimation. In this work, we present a predictive tracking approach that propagates positional updates between multiple tracked landmarks to obtain a global pose prediction. This enables more robust tracking under high velocities. Our results demonstrate RMSEs below 1 mm for velocities up to 100 mm/s and up to nine consecutively tracked landmarks, highlighting the potential of global motion propagation and prediction for improving the robustness of OCT-based tracking.

</details>


### [42] [MC-DeTra: Motion-Consistent Joint Object Detection and Socially-Aware Trajectory Forecasting in Bird's-Eye-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11717)
*Vladislav Diuzhev,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对统一感知-预测的自动驾驶模型，公开复现了最强的统一模型 DeTra（原本无公开实现），并提出 MC-DeTra：一组仅在训练时生效的"运动一致性"机制，通过辅助监督信号与输出间一致性约束改善共享 BEV 表征，在不增加推理延迟的前提下提升动态、社会化场景下的轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一检测与轨迹预测的模型旨在将感知与预测融合，直接在由 LiDAR 与高精地图栅格化的共享鸟瞰图（BEV）上回归 actor 轨迹。然而，这类模型对动态、移动目标的预测精度仍是最困难的部分，而性能最强的统一模型 DeTra 并未公开实现，导致无法复现与比较。论文的动机是：1）提供首个公开的 DeTra 复现及近似实现说明；2）在共享 BEV 表征上引入额外监督，缓解动态 actor 预测精度不足的问题，且不引入任何推理开销。

Method: 在复现的 DeTra 基础上提出 MC-DeTra，属于一组运动一致性（motion-consistency）机制，核心为三类信号在训练阶段对共享 BEV 表征施加监督：1）每个 actor 观测到的历史运动（past motion）作为辅助监督信号；2）周围交通流的占据（occupancy），用以刻画其"社会上下文"（social context）；3）一个输出间一致性约束，将 actor 的预测朝向（heading）与其预测的运动方向（direction of motion）对齐。所有新增损失均为 train-only 且 inference-safe：仅用于训练时塑造共享 BEV 表征，测试阶段移除，因此不增加推理延迟。此外，论文基于梯度进行了损失标定（loss-calibration）分析，以揭示辅助目标在共享骨干网络上的相互竞争关系。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 上，采用严格的、检测条件化的（detection-conditioned）预测协议进行评估。结果表明：MC-DeTra 在保持或提升检测精度的同时，改善了动态、具备社会交互属性的场景下的轨迹预测性能；基于梯度的损失标定分析揭示了辅助目标在共享骨干处的相互竞争；消融实验进一步指出哪些信号贡献最大。论文公开了代码、配置与评估工具（https://github.com/diuzhevVlad/MC-DeTra）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：1）首个公开的 DeTra 复现，并记录了近似实现细节；2）提出 MC-DeTra 系列运动一致性机制，通过历史运动、社会语境占据以及朝向-运动方向一致性三类 train-only 信号，在无推理延迟代价下提升统一 BEV 模型对动态目标的轨迹预测；3）提供损失标定与消融分析，增强了方法的可解释性。潜在局限：方案依赖 DeTra 复现的正确性与近似精度（可能与原实现存在差异）；评估仅在 Waymo Open Dataset 单一数据集上进行；辅助信号的收益依赖于标注质量与可用性；三类信号间的竞争关系需要进一步标定，泛化性有待更多数据集与场景验证。

Abstract: Unified models for object detection and trajectory forecasting aim to merge perception and prediction for autonomous driving, refining actor trajectories directly over shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) images rasterized from LiDAR and high-definition maps. Their accuracy on dynamic, moving actors, however, remains the hardest part of the task, and the strongest such model, DeTra, has no public implementation. We contribute an openly released DeTra reimplementation with documented approximations, and on top of it MC-DeTra: a family of motion-consistency mechanisms that add supervision through two annotation-derived auxiliary signals -- each actor's observed past motion and the occupancy of the surrounding traffic that forms its social context -- and one inter-output consistency constraint that aligns an actor's predicted heading with its predicted direction of motion. Every proposed loss is train-only and inference-safe: it shapes the shared BEV representation during training and is removed at test time, adding no inference latency. On the Waymo Open Dataset, evaluated under a strict, detection-conditioned forecasting protocol, MC-DeTra improves dynamic, socially-situated trajectory forecasting while preserving or improving detection accuracy; a gradient-based loss-calibration analysis exposes how the auxiliary objectives compete at the shared backbone, and our ablation identifies which signals contribute most. We release code, configurations, and evaluation tooling at https://github.com/diuzhevVlad/MC-DeTra.

</details>


### [43] [R4Tun: LLM-guided adaptive segmental tunnel lining segmentation in point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11360)
*Xinghui Tao,Zehao Ye,Guangming Wang,Jelena Ninić,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R4Tun 是一个由大语言模型（LLM）驱动的有界参数自适应框架，用于在专家设计的隧道衬砌点云分割流水线（SAM4Tun）之上进行条件自适应的参数调节。它通过引入结构化上下文——记忆(m)、状态(s)、知识(k)——引导 LLM 输出可审计的有界参数变更，在 Seg2Tunnel 数据集上显著提升了分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管片式隧道衬砌的自动化巡检需要从3D点云中进行自适应分割，但专家手工调参的流水线在隧道工况（如断面几何、光照/噪声、结构类型）发生变化时往往性能急剧退化。传统的静态配置难以泛化到多样化场景，而重新为每种工况人工调参代价高昂且依赖专家经验。作者的目标是构建一种无需人工标注（label-free）、可控且跨 LLM 一致的自动适配机制，让流水线在复杂工况下仍能维持较高的分割精度。

Method: 方法建立在已有专家流水线 SAM4Tun 之上。R4Tun 的核心设计是：以结构化上下文 $m+s+k$ 作为提示输入——记忆(m)提供历史调参经验，状态(s)描述当前隧道/点云的具体条件，知识(k)注入专家先验与参数语义约束。LLM 在该上下文下进行"有界参数调参"（bounded parameter tuning），即只能在预设范围内输出参数改动，从而避免无约束的漂移。每次参数变更都有日志化的理由（logged rationales），保证可审计性。整个流程标签无关（label-free），并作为一个"机制贡献"而非可部署的最终巡检系统来定位。

Result: 在 Seg2Tunnel 数据集的30个子集上评估（13个规则、17个复杂子集），使用3种不同 LLM，共进行 270 次运行（30 隧道 × 3 LLM × 3 上下文设置）。完整 $m+s+k$ 设计将平均交并比（mIoU）从静态 SAM4Tun 基线的 0.18 提升到 0.43–0.48，总体精度（OA）从 0.42 提升到 0.59–0.65。在最接近参考标准的规则（交错/错缝）子集上，mIoU 达到 0.784–0.796。跨 LLM 结果显示出相似的参数调整趋势（平均增益的 95% 置信区间重叠），且不同 LLM 一致地调整了同一组关键参数。主要指标为 mIoU 与 OA。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出 R4Tun 这一由 LLM 驱动的可控、标签无关、跨 LLM 一致的参数自适应机制，在 SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel 设定下实现了稳定且显著的精度提升，并因其日志化理由而具备可审计性。其意义在于为 3D 点云分割流水线的条件自适应提供了一种新范式。局限在于：作者明确将其定位为机制贡献而非可部署的最终巡检系统；评估仅局限于 SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel 这一特定流水线与数据集组合，跨 LLM 差异虽一致但仍存在，泛化到其他任务与场景尚待验证。

Abstract: Automated inspection of segmental tunnel linings requires adaptive segmentation from 3D point clouds, yet expert-tuned pipelines often degrade when tunnel conditions vary. This paper presents R4Tun, a large language model (LLM)-driven adaptation framework that extends an expert-designed pipeline (SAM4Tun) with bounded parameter tuning informed by structured context: memory ($m$), state ($s$), and knowledge ($k$). Evaluated on 30 selected Seg2Tunnel subsets (13 regular, 17 complex) across three LLMs, the full $m+s+k$ design raised mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) from 0.18 to 0.43--0.48 and overall accuracy (OA) from 0.42 to 0.59--0.65 relative to the static SAM4Tun baseline, with the near-reference regular (staggered) subsets reaching mIoU 0.784--0.796 across LLMs. Across 270 (30 tunnels $\times$ 3 different LLMs $\times$ 3 context settings) runs, the LLMs showed similar parameter-adjustment trends (with overlapping 95\% CIs on mean gains) and consistently adjusted a shared set of critical parameters. These results support R4Tun as a controlled, label-free, cross-LLM adaptation mechanism in the tested SAM4Tun--Seg2Tunnel setting, demonstrating consistent accuracy gains; we position R4Tun as a mechanism contribution rather than a deployable final-inspection system, in which each bounded parameter change is auditable via logged rationales.

</details>


### [44] [Vision Transformer-Based Multi-Level Feature Fusion for Multi-Label Sewer Defect Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11375)
*Xu Fang,Zhuoran Wang,Qing Li,Shengyu Zhang,Guanzhi Deng,Jianbiao He,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文面向下水道缺陷的多标签自动分类任务，提出层次化视觉Transformer模型Sewer-Transformer-ML（含多级特征融合），并给出两个轻量化架构Sewer-MobileNet-ML与Sewer-Mobile-TransNet，以在资源受限的巡检场景中平衡精度与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下水道缺陷的自动化分类对基础设施状况评估与维护决策至关重要。然而现有深度学习方法在大规模多标签场景下难以兼顾分类精度与计算复杂度：高精度模型往往参数庞大、难以部署于资源受限的检测平台，而轻量模型又牺牲了分类性能。因此需要在统一框架下探索高精度与轻量化的协同设计。

Method: 方法核心为层次化视觉Transformer Sewer-Transformer-ML，采用多级特征融合机制聚合不同层级的特征表示。在此基础上设计两个轻量架构：Sewer-MobileNet-ML（基于轻量CNN）与Sewer-Mobile-TransNet（CNN与Transformer混合）。论文还针对不同骨干特性研究了特征融合策略：实验表明Transformer特征更适合直接拼接（direct concatenation），而多尺度CNN特征更适合基于注意力的融合（attention-based fusion）。此外，讨论了在Sewer-ML上预训练对下游小样本任务的迁移效果。

Result: 在Sewer-ML测试集上，Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base取得F2_CIW为65.68%、F1_Normal为92.68%，在公开排行榜上排名第一，F2_CIW领先第二名7.6个百分点。Sewer-MobileNet-ML在仅约17M参数下达到65.73%的F2_CIW，相对基础模型参数减少约95%。在标准Sewer-Capsule数据划分下，Sewer-Mobile-TransNet取得96.43%的分类准确率。当训练集缩减至1,177张图像时，Sewer-ML预训练持续提升模型性能。消融实验验证了上述融合策略的有效性。评价指标包括F2_CIW、F1_Normal与分类准确率。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出高精度的层次化视觉Transformer多标签分类框架与两个面向资源受限部署的轻量模型，并在Sewer-ML与Sewer-Capsule上验证了精度与效率的平衡，同时给出了针对不同骨干的特征融合设计准则与预训练迁移证据，为自动化下水道巡检、轻量模型设计及跨民用基础设施巡检平台适配提供了计算依据。潜在局限包括：轻量模型的最佳融合策略依赖经验验证、跨数据集与小样本场景的泛化能力仍受预训练数据规模影响。

Abstract: Automated classification of sewer defects is essential for infrastructure condition assessment and maintenance decision-making, but existing deep learning methods struggle to balance classification accuracy and computational complexity in large-scale multi-label scenarios. This study develops Sewer-Transformer-ML, a hierarchical vision Transformer with multi-level feature fusion, together with two lightweight architectures, Sewer-MobileNet-ML and Sewer-Mobile-TransNet, for resource-constrained inspection scenarios. On the Sewer-ML test set, Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.68% and an $F1_{\text{Normal}}$ of 92.68%, ranking first on the public leaderboard and exceeding the second-ranked method by 7.6 percentage points in $F2_{\text{CIW}}$. Sewer-MobileNet-ML achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.73% with only 17 M parameters, representing an approximately 95% parameter reduction relative to the base model. Under the standard Sewer-Capsule data split, Sewer-Mobile-TransNet achieved 96.43% classification accuracy. When the training set was reduced to 1,177 images, pretraining on Sewer-ML consistently improved model performance. Ablation experiments further showed that direct concatenation was more effective for Transformer features, whereas attention-based fusion better supported multiscale CNN features. These findings provide a computational basis for automated sewer inspection, lightweight model design, and adaptation across civil infrastructure inspection platforms.

</details>


### [45] [Brain-PACE: A Deep Siamese MRI Framework for Modelling Longitudinal Brain Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11378)
*Samuel Maddox,Jacob Newman,Saber Sami,Michal Mackiewicz,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,the Australian Imaging Biomarkers,Lifestyle flagship study of ageing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Brain-PACE 是一个从配对纵向 T1 加权 MRI 直接估计"结构性脑老化速度"的 Siamese 框架，扩展自 LILAC，结合空间注意力、软标签分布学习与 Cramér 距离目标。它能识别轻度认知障碍（MCI）中42.6%个体的加速老化，且其预测速度与认知/功能损害及区域 tau 负荷相关，优于由重复横截面脑龄差间接推算的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄（brain age）已成为评估脑健康与疾病的常用代理指标，但纵向上的脑老化"轨迹/速度"仍定义不清，临床价值受限。传统做法通常基于重复的横截面脑龄估计再做差，间接推算老化速度，可能丢失与持续病理变化相关的互补信息。因此需要直接对配对纵向 MRI 建模老化的"节奏（pace）"，以更敏感地刻画早期神经退行性改变。

Method: 方法基于已有的 Siamese 纵向框架（LILAC）扩展：输入为配对 T1 加权 MRI，直接估计 Brain-Predicted Age Acceleration（Brain-PACE），即老化速度。技术上有三点创新：(1) 引入空间注意力机制以聚焦相关脑区；(2) 采用软标签分布学习（soft label distribution learning）建模脑龄输出分布而非单点回归；(3) 使用 Cramér 距离作为训练目标，替代常规回归损失，从而提升概率预测性能、降低预测偏差，并能输出预测不确定性度量。

Result: 在配对 T1 加权 MRI 数据上评估。Brain-PACE 在 42.6% 的 MCI 参与者中识别出加速老化。更快的 Brain-PACE 与更大的功能与认知损害相关（FAQ r=0.35；ADAS13 r=0.30；CDR-SB r=0.32），并与后扣带（r=0.59）、楔前叶（r=0.47）和内嗅皮层（r=0.37）的区域 tau 负荷相关。这些关联强于由重复横截面脑龄估计间接推算的速度，说明直接纵向建模捕捉到与持续病理改变相关的互补信息。方法上，Cramér 距离目标提升了概率性能并减小预测偏差。

Conclusion: Brain-PACE 提出直接的纵向脑老化速度估计框架，通过空间注意力、软标签分布学习与 Cramér 距离目标改进概率预测并提供不确定性度量；实验证明其与认知功能损害及区域 tau 负荷显著相关，且优于间接推算方式，支持其作为对早期神经退行性变敏感的互补性纵向影像表型。局限方面：摘要未报告样本规模、队列构成、具体模型精度指标或外部验证，其临床普适性与泛化能力仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Brain age estimation has become a popular research proxy for assessing brain health and disease, yet longitudinal trajectories of brain ageing are still poorly defined, and clinical use is limited. Building on existing Siamese longitudinal frameworks, we develop Brain-Predicted Age Acceleration (Brain-PACE) to directly estimate the pace of structural brain ageing from paired T1-weighted MRI. Brain-PACE identified accelerated ageing in $42.6$% of participants with mild cognitive impairment. Faster Brain-PACE was associated with greater functional and cognitive impairment (FAQ; $r=0.35$, ADAS13; $r=0.30$, CDR-SB; $r=0.32$) and greater regional tau burden in the posterior cingulate ($r=0.59$), precuneus ($r=0.47$), and entorhinal cortex ($r=0.37$). These associations were stronger than those observed when pace was calculated indirectly from repeated cross-sectional brain age estimates, suggesting that direct longitudinal modelling captures complementary information relevant to ongoing pathological change. Methodologically, Brain-PACE extends the LILAC framework by combining spatial attention with soft label distribution learning and a Cramér distance objective, improving probabilistic performance and reducing prediction bias while providing measures of predictive uncertainty. Together, these findings support Brain-PACE as a complementary longitudinal imaging phenotype with sensitivity to relevant clinical and biological changes in early neurodegeneration.

</details>


### [46] [DINO-Med: A Unified Patch-Based Adaptation Framework for Multi-Modal Medical Image Analysis Applied to Liver Fibrosis Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11380)
*Boya Wang,Ruizhe Li,Chao Chen,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的、基于patch的多模态医学影像框架，通过免训练配准、自动定位和mask过滤的patch提取，并对四种patch级特征表示进行受控对比，其中冻结的DINOv3特征在肝纤维化分期任务上显著优于Radiomics、ResNet和SAM-Med2D基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然图像基础模型（如DINOv3）迁移到多模态医学影像面临显著的域差距：自然彩色图像与多通道医学扫描在通道数、灰度分布和纹理语义上差异明显。论文旨在以肝纤维化分期为案例，系统评估不同patch级特征表示在跨模态医学影像上的有效性，探索如何复用预训练基础模型而不需大规模微调。

Method: 提出统一的patch-based框架，流程包括：(1) 免训练配准（training-free registration）；(2) 自动病灶/区域定位；(3) 基于mask过滤的patch提取；最后通过分层策略将patch级特征聚合为受试者（subject）级诊断。对比四种patch级特征：手工设计的Radiomics、有监督学习的ResNet、医学基础模型SAM-Med2D特征、以及冻结（frozen）的DINOv3特征。为保证公平对比，所有特征均接入同一轻量MLP分类头，并在刚性配准与可变形配准两种设置下评估。训练策略仅使用轻度纤维化（S1）与肝硬化（S4）两类，使单个分类器同时完成显著纤维化检测与肝硬化分期。

Result: 在CARE 2025 Liver Track 4队列的360名受试者上，采用10组随机90%/10%训练-测试划分进行评估。DINOv3-based框架显著优于所有基线，在S1类上取得最佳分类准确率78.4%，在S4类上取得75.8%；同时覆盖刚性配准与可变形配准两种设置，验证了冻结基础模型特征在下游医学分类任务中的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：验证冻结DINOv3特征结合轻量MLP头可在多模态医学影像分级任务中超越手工特征、有监督CNN特征及医学专用基础模型，并提供一个免训练配准+patch聚合的统一框架。局限在于仅在单一肝纤维化数据集与两类（S1/S4）任务上验证，跨器官、跨模态及细粒度多级分期的泛化能力有待进一步验证；同时未对基础模型进行领域适配或微调，域差距的残余影响尚未充分探讨。

Abstract: Adapting natural-image foundation models like DINOv3 to multi-modal medical imaging is challenging due to the significant domain gap between natural color images and multi-channel medical scans. We present a unified, patch-based framework that processes raw multimodal imaging through training-free registration, automated localization, and mask-filtered patch extraction. This architecture culminates in a hierarchical strategy that aggregates patch-level insights into subject-level diagnostics. Using liver fibrosis staging as a case study, we evaluate four patch-level feature representations: handcrafted Radiomics features, learned ResNet features, pre-trained foundation model SAM-Med2D features, and frozen DINOv3 features. To ensure a controlled comparison, all models utilize the same lightweight MLP head and are evaluated across both rigid and deformable registration settings. Our training protocol focuses on mild fibrosis (S1) and cirrhosis (S4) classes only, enabling a single classifier to address both substantial fibrosis detection and cirrhosis staging. Evaluated via 10 random train (90%)/ test (10%) splits on 360 subjects from the CARE 2025 Liver Track 4 cohort, our DINOv3-based framework significantly outperforms all baselines, achieving the best classification accuracy of 78.4% for S1 and 75.8% for S4.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-Modal Controlled Coherent Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11439)
*Yifei Liu,Qiong Cao,Hongwei Yi,Huaiguang Jiang,Changxing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOCO 是一个基于扩散模型的多模态 3D 人形动作生成框架，能够同时接受语音音频、文本描述和轨迹数据等多种并行输入，通过"解耦-组合-再扩散"的迭代方式生成连贯自然的全身动作，且无需对齐的多模态训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以自然地边走路边说话，即同时执行由多种模态驱动的复合行为。然而，现有的 3D 虚拟人动作生成方法受限于对齐多模态数据的稀缺，通常采用"顺序拼接"或"加权求和"的方式组合来自不同模态的动作。这种策略容易导致各部位动作之间不匹配、整体动作不自然或不真实，无法实现真正的多模态共现行为生成。因此，如何在缺乏对齐多模态数据的条件下生成协调同步的多模态驱动动作，是一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 作者提出 MOCO，一个基于扩散模型的多模态动作生成框架，核心创新在于对动作生成过程的"解耦"。在扩散模型的每一步去噪中，模型对每种模态（语音音频、文本描述、轨迹）从输入噪声中独立生成各自的运动，然后依据预定义的空间规则将这些身体部位的动作用进行组装（例如语音驱动上半身手势、轨迹驱动下半身行走）。组装后的复合动作会被重新加噪（扩散）并作为下一去噪步骤的输入噪声。通过这种迭代式的"独立生成→空间组装→再扩散"循环，每种模态能够在上一步整体动作的上下文中不断调整和细化自身贡献，从而逐步实现跨模态的动作协调。该方法的关键优点在于不依赖对齐的多模态数据集，即可实现多模态输入的联合建模。

Result: 作者使用专门构建的多模态基准（multimodal benchmark）对 MOCO 进行评估。实验结果表明，MOCO 在效果上优于现有基线方法，能够生成更加连贯、自然且跨模态同步的 3D 虚拟人动作，推动了面向 3D 虚拟人的多模态动作生成研究方向的发展。摘要中未给出具体的数据集规模、评价指标（如 FID、多样性、多模态一致性等）和数值结果。

Conclusion: MOCO 的主要贡献在于提出了一种无需对齐多模态数据的扩散式多模态动作生成框架，通过将动作生成解耦并在去噪过程中按空间规则组装与迭代细化，实现了语音、文本和轨迹三者的协调统一，克服了传统顺序拼接或加权求和的缺陷。其意义在于推进了 3D 虚拟人的自然多模态行为生成。可能存在的局限性包括：方法依赖人工预定义的空间组装规则，可能限制动作表达的灵活性与泛化性；迭代式再扩散可能带来较高的计算开销；文本中未提供定量指标与消融实验细节，其有效性和可扩展性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: It is natural for humans to walk and talk simultaneously. This paper tackles the challenge of replicating such natural behaviors in 3D avatar motion generation driven by concurrent multimodal inputs, such as a text description of a man walking alongside speech audio. Existing methods, constrained by the scarcity of aligned multimodal data, typically combine motions from individual modalities sequentially or through weighted sums. However, they often result in mismatched or unrealistic movements. To overcome these limitations, we propose MOCO, a novel diffusion-based framework capable of processing multiple simultaneous inputs, including speech audio, text descriptions, and trajectory data, to generate coherent and lifelike motions without requiring aligned multimodal data. Our key innovation lies in decoupling the motion generation process. During each denoising step, the diffusion model independently generates motions for each modality from the input noise and assembles the body parts according to predefined spatial rules. The resulting combined motion is then diffused and serves as the input noise for the subsequent denoising step. This iterative approach enables each modality to refine its contribution within the context of the overall motion, progressively harmonizing movements across modalities. Consequently, the generated motions become increasingly natural and fluid with each iteration, achieving coherent and synchronized behaviors. We evaluate our approach using a purpose-built multimodal benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate that MOCO outperforms existing baselines, advancing the field of multimodal motion generation for 3D avatars.

</details>


### [48] [BruNet: A Cross-Domain Transfer Framework for Bruise Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11463)
*Qiming Wang,Richard J. Motley,Ebube E. Obi,Xianfang Sun,Paul L. Rosin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BruNet 是一个用于淤伤（bruise）分割的分割框架，将基于 ViT 的视觉编码器（自监督 DINOv3 或预训练 LingBot-Vision）与 SAM 掩码解码器结合，在 HAM10000 皮肤病变数据集上训练，并在独立的淤伤数据集上零微调跨域评估，声称是首个面向自动淤伤像素级分割的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淤伤分割在医学影像中具有挑战性：数据与标注稀缺、边界弥散（diffuse boundaries）、外观高度多变。现有针对淤伤的机器学习与计算机视觉研究主要聚焦于检测、分类或颜色分析，而非像素级定位（pixel-level localisation）。因此，自动淤伤分割这一任务此前基本未被系统研究，作者将其视为首个相关工作，动机在于填补从“是否/何种淤伤”到“淤伤在哪、范围多大”的分割空白，并验证跨域泛化能力。

Method: BruNet 采用编码器-解码器式的分割框架：视觉编码器为 ViT 结构，可选自监督的 DINOv3 或预训练的 LingBot-Vision 骨干；解码器为基于 SAM 的掩码解码器（mask decoder）。关键在于利用大规模预训练视觉表征与通用分割解码器的组合，实现从皮肤病变数据到淤伤数据的跨域迁移，且在目标域不进行额外微调。模型在 HAM10000 皮肤病变数据集上训练，随后直接在独立的淤伤数据集上评估。

Result: 实验在 HAM10000 上训练、在单独的淤伤数据集上零微调评估，以测试跨域泛化。对比对象包括 CNN 类模型、当前最先进的分割模型、ChatGPT-4o/5 辅助的 SAM2 零样本基线，以及面向医学的 MedSAM。结果显示 BruNet 在上述对比中表现更优，表明其在淤伤分割上具有较强的跨域泛化能力。摘要未给出具体分割指标（如 Dice/IoU）数值、数据规模或消融细节。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出 BruNet 框架并宣称是首个针对自动淤伤分割的研究，通过 ViT 编码器与 SAM 解码器的结合并借助皮肤病变数据训练，展示了优于 CNN、SOTA 分割模型、零样本 SAM2/ChatGPT 辅助基线和 MedSAM 的跨域泛化性能。可能的局限包括：摘要未报告定量指标、淤伤数据集规模与标注质量未说明、训练集（HAM10000）与目标域（淤伤）存在显著域差异、以及缺乏消融以厘清 DINOv3 与 LingBot-Vision 各自贡献和零微调设定的边界条件。

Abstract: Segmenting bruises is a challenging task in medical imaging due to limited data and annotations, diffuse boundaries, and highly variable appearance. In this work, we propose BruNet, a segmentation framework that combines a ViT-based visual encoder (a self-supervised DINOv3 or a pretrained LingBot-Vision backbone) with a SAM-based mask decoder. BruNet is trained on the HAM10000 skin lesion dataset and evaluated on a separate bruise dataset without additional fine-tuning. Although a small number of prior studies have explored machine learning and computer vision for bruise analysis, existing work has primarily focused on detection, classification, or colour analysis rather than pixel-level localisation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to address automatic bruise segmentation. Our results show that BruNet outperforms CNN-based models, state-of-the-art segmentation models, ChatGPT-4o/5-assisted SAM2 zero-shot baselines, and the medical-oriented MedSAM model, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalisation to bruise segmentation.

</details>


### [49] [BridgeMatch: Conditional Transport Bridges in Matching Matrix Space for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11472)
*Qianliang Wu,Haobo Jiang,Guangwei Gao,Shuo Chen,Jin Xie,Jian Yang,Yaqing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种两阶段生成式求解器（\\paper），在粗细两个分辨率上都保持完整的软匹配矩阵，避免粗到精方法因top-K剪枝丢失弱但正确的假设。Stage I 用去噪扩散在粗分辨率空间估计全局匹配矩阵，再按层级提升到高分辨率（得到秩受限、块常数的提升矩阵）；Stage II 通过条件传输桥（确定性CFM ODE或随机Brownian-bridge SDE）进行细化。在4DMatch/4DLoMatch上两种变体均优于对比方法，并在CAPE与DeepDeform上无需目标域自适应即可提升跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的非刚性点云对应关系对可变形解剖配准、具身感知与操作、动态3D重建都至关重要，但也是一个难题。现有的粗到精（coarse-to-fine）方法虽能降低计算开销，做法却是仅保留top-K粗区域进行精细匹配。这种剪枝会删除那些"弱但正确"的假设，使精细匹配被限制在一个不完整的搜索空间中，从而降低对应精度，尤其在低重叠（low-overlap）情形下更为严重。因此作者希望用生成式建模的方式在不牺牲完整性的前提下实现高效的两分辨率匹配。

Method: 方法为两阶段生成式求解器，始终保留完整的软匹配矩阵：(1) Stage I 在紧凑的粗分辨率空间中用去噪扩散（denoising diffusion）估计全局匹配矩阵；(2) 随后将该矩阵提升（lift）到高分辨率，同时保持其层级结构，得到的提升矩阵具有秩受限（rank-bounded）与块常数（block-constant）的性质；(3) Stage II 通过条件传输桥（conditional transport bridge）对该提升矩阵进行细化，实现两种动力学形式：一种是确定性的、以端点为参数的条件流匹配（CFM）ODE；另一种是受Schrödinger桥启发的随机Brownian-bridge SDE。两种变体共享同一提升源矩阵、一个时间条件化的Transformer（time-conditioned transformer）以及匹配矩阵端点预测器。

Result: 在4DMatch与4DLoMatch基准上，两种变体（CFM ODE 与 Brownian-bridge SDE）产生的对应关系都比对比方法更准确，并改善了下游配准效果，在低重叠情形下增益更大。此外，方法在CAPE与DeepDeform上提升了跨数据集泛化能力，且无需目标域自适应，使用的是同一个变形求解器。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出了一种基于生成式建模的两阶段非刚体点云匹配框架，用"粗分辨率扩散估计+层级提升+条件传输桥细化"的完整软匹配矩阵范式取代了会丢失正确假设的top-K剪枝策略，并给出了确定性ODE与随机SDE两种实现。方法在多个基准上提升精度与跨域泛化。潜在局限包括两阶段生成式流程的计算开销、扩散与桥过程带来的推理步数与超参敏感性，以及低重叠下增益与"完整软匹配矩阵"设计之间因果关系的进一步验证（摘要未给出收敛性、时间开销或可扩展性方面的量化结果）。

Abstract: Reliable non-rigid point cloud correspondences are important for deformable anatomical registration, embodied perception and manipulation, and dynamic 3D reconstruction. Coarse-to-fine methods reduce computational cost by selecting the top-\(K\) coarse regions. However, this pruning may remove weak but correct hypotheses and restrict fine matching to an incomplete search space. We present \paper, a two-stage generative solver that maintains the complete soft matching matrix at both coarse and high resolutions. Stage~I uses denoising diffusion to estimate a global matching matrix in the compact coarse-resolution space. We then lift this matrix to high resolution while preserving its hierarchy. The lifted matrix is rank-bounded and block-constant. Stage~II refines it through a conditional transport bridge. We implement the bridge with two types of dynamics: a deterministic endpoint-parameterized conditional Flow Matching (CFM) ODE and a stochastic Brownian-bridge SDE inspired by Schrödinger bridges. Both variants share the lifted source, a time-conditioned transformer, and a matching-matrix endpoint predictor. Experiments on 4DMatch and 4DLoMatch show that both variants produce more accurate correspondences than the compared methods and improve downstream registration, with larger gains in low-overlap cases. They also improve cross-dataset generalization on CAPE and DeepDeform without target-domain adaptation while using the same deformation solver.

</details>


### [50] [Pre- and Post-Treatment Brain Metastases Segmentation Using nnU-Net with Post-Processing for BraTS 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11477)
*Haobin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个面向 BraTS 2026 Task 1（脑转移瘤）体积分割的务实流水线：基于 5 折 nnU-Net ResEnc-L 集成模型，叠加面向病变级 Dice（LW-DSC）的规则式后处理级联，并在官方验证榜上取得 ET/TC/WT/RC 分别为 0.733/0.751/0.713/0.549 的 LW-DSC；同时通过无泄漏的五折 OOF 分析审计每一后处理阶段的真实增益，并给出对 LW-DSC 指标的机理分析和十三项失败（负）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑转移瘤在病灶大小、强化模式和治疗后外观上存在高度病变间异质性，同时覆盖治疗前与治疗后病例的体积分割是 BraTS 2026 Task 1 的核心难题。传统上此类挑战赛的排行榜提升可能来自过拟合验证集的后处理技巧，缺乏可复现性；此外 LW-DSC 这类检测导向指标与全局 Dice 的行为差异很大，需要理解其机制以指导后处理设计。

Method: 方法主体为 5 折 nnU-Net ResEnc-L 集成：每折在 1,296 例四模态训练数据上独立训练 1,000 epoch，使用标准 Dice + 交叉熵损失。集成之上叠加针对 LW-DSC 调优的规则式后处理级联（含召回恢复型操作与连通分量删除等）。评估不仅依赖官方验证排行榜，还采用五折 out-of-fold（OOF）分析，在全部 1,296 例训练数据上、无模型训练泄漏地审计每一后处理阶段，并使用官方 BraTS_evaluation 代码打分，以区分真实稳健增益与仅排行榜增益。论文还提供 LW-DSC 的机理分析，解释为何召回恢复型后处理风险低，而组件删除风险高。

Result: 在官方验证榜上，最终流水线在 ET/TC/WT/RC 四个子区域取得 LW-DSC 0.733/0.751/0.713/0.549。五折 OOF 审计确认，后处理级联中仅两个阶段是稳健且逐折一致的改进，第三个阶段只在排行榜上提升、OOF 无法复现。作者另报告十三项负结果，覆盖损失函数设计、替代骨干网络与推理时设置，其中若干与常见直觉相悖。评估使用官方 BraTS_evaluation 代码与 LW-DSC（病变级 Dice 相似系数）指标。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献有三：一是构建了在 BraTS 2026 脑转移瘤分割任务上有效的 nnU-Net 集成加后处理流水线；二是强调以无泄漏 OOF 审计替代盲目信任排行榜，揭示排行榜增益与真实泛化增益的差异；三是给出 LW-DSC 的机理分析并提供十三项负结果。意义在于为医学分割挑战赛提供更严谨的评估范式，并对后处理策略（召回恢复 vs. 组件删除）给出可迁移的经验。局限在于方法以 nnU-Net 与规则式后处理为主，创新性偏工程性；OOF 审计严格性依赖折划分；负结果多为经验性结论，其普适性尚需在更多数据集与指标上验证。源码以 Apache-2.0 在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Brain metastases exhibit high inter-lesion variability in size, enhancement pattern, and post-treatment appearance, making volumetric segmentation of both pre- and post-treatment cases the central challenge of the BraTS 2026 Task 1 (Brain Metastases). We build a pragmatic pipeline on a 5-fold nnU-Net ResEnc-L ensemble, in which each fold is trained independently for 1,000 epochs with the standard Dice + cross-entropy loss on 1,296 four-modality training cases. This ensemble is followed by a rule-based post-processing cascade tuned for the lesion-wise Dice similarity coefficient (LW-DSC), a detection-oriented metric that behaves very differently from the traditional global Dice. The final pipeline reaches an LW-DSC of 0.733 / 0.751 / 0.713 / 0.549 on the enhancing tumour (ET), tumour core (TC), whole tumour (WT), and resection cavity (RC) sub-regions on the official validation leaderboard. Rather than trusting these leaderboard gains, we audit every post-processing stage with a five-fold out-of-fold (OOF) analysis with no model-training leakage over all 1,296 training cases, scored with the official BraTS evaluation code (BraTS_evaluation): it confirms two stages as robust, per-fold-consistent improvements while the third improves only the leaderboard and does not reproduce out-of-fold. We further provide a mechanistic analysis of the LW-DSC metric that explains why recall-recovering post-processing carries low risk whereas component deletion does not, and we report thirteen negative results spanning loss engineering, alternative backbones, and inference-time settings, several of which run counter to widely held intuitions. Source code is released under Apache-2.0 at https://github.com/hornbeamliu/brats2026-met.

</details>


### [51] [FreeFlow: A Bias-free Hierarchical Transformer for Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11486)
*Vladislav Bargatin,Alexander Yakovenko,Khaled Abud,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeFlow 提出一种不含任何光流专用组件（如相关体积、特征 warp、迭代精化）的分层 Transformer 光流估计框架，仅用单一前馈编码器-解码器，通过融合窗口注意力、移位窗口注意力和低分辨率全局注意力实现 SOTA 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光流方法依赖任务特定的归纳偏置（correlation volumes、feature warping、iterative refinement 等）来获得高精度。这些偏置虽有效，但将模型限制在预设启发式内，削弱了模型表达能力，并导致流程复杂、计算开销增加。论文的核心动机是验证：不借助任何光流专用组件，仅凭通用视觉 Transformer 架构能否达到甚至超越 SOTA 光流精度，并保持良好可扩展性与内存效率。

Method: FreeFlow 采用分层（hierarchical）Transformer 架构，为纯前馈 encoder–decoder，不含相关体积、warp 或迭代精化模块。其核心是组合三种注意力变体：(1) 窗口注意力（window attention）负责局部细粒度建模；(2) 移位窗口注意力（shifted-window attention）实现跨窗口信息交互，扩大有效感受野；(3) 在降采样分辨率上运行的全局注意力（global attention），以低计算成本捕获长程依赖。该设计使模型容量可自然扩展，从 small 到 large 变体获得持续一致的精度提升。

Result: 在主流光流基准上取得 SOTA 结果：Sintel（Clean 0.68 / Final 1.48 EPE）、KITTI-2015（3.23 Fl-all）、Spring（3.192 1px）。模型在 1080p 分辨率推理下仍保持内存高效。模型从小到大的扩展带来一致精度增益，验证了架构的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明无需光流专用归纳偏置的通用分层 Transformer 即可达到 SOTA 光流估计性能，简化了流程并提升了可扩展性与内存效率。局限方面：论文摘要未明确定量对比推理速度/延迟，仅提及内存效率；未说明在极端位移、遮挡或弱纹理等困难场景下的表现，也未提及训练数据规模与训练成本。

Abstract: Optical flow methods typically rely on task-specific inductive biases, such as correlation volumes, feature warping, and iterative refinement, among others, to reach high accuracy. While effective, such biases constrain the model to predefined heuristics, which can limit its expressivity and lead to more complex pipelines and additional computational cost. We present FreeFlow, a hierarchical transformer built without any flow-specific components, using instead a single feed-forward encoder--decoder. FreeFlow combines three attention variants: window attention for local processing, shifted-window attention for cross-window information exchange, and a global attention operating at a reduced resolution. The resulting architecture scales naturally with model capacity, enabling a consistent accuracy gain from small to large variants. Despite the absence of standard inductive biases, FreeFlow achieves state-of-the-art results on major benchmarks, including Sintel (0.68/1.48 EPE on Clean/Final), KITTI-2015 (3.23 Fl-all), and Spring (3.192 1px), while remaining memory efficient at 1080p inference.

</details>


### [52] [Recursive Code World Models: Building Complex Worlds through Recursive Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11499)
*Zhiqi Li,Yuxuan Liao,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RCWM（Recursive Code World Models）提出一种从单张参考图像递归重建复杂3D可执行世界的框架，将递归场景程序（RSP）表示与一个递归调用自身的构建求解器结合，通过"整体—局部—整体"的递归构造原则，在复杂场景的代码化图像到场景重建任务上超越已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码化世界模型（code world models）把世界表示为可执行程序，但这种表示本身并不能回答"如何构建一个复杂世界"的问题。现有方法在面对复杂3D场景时，缺乏一个结构化的构造策略来同时保证场景级几何/关系一致性与细粒度局部结构的精度。论文的动机是：像人类或程序化建模那样，先建立整体框架，再递归重建尚未解决的部分，最后回到整体去修正局部与全局的耦合关系，从而在单一参考图像条件下重建复杂的可执行3D世界。

Method: 核心是将可执行世界表示为递归场景程序（RSP），即由可组合的场景代码构成的层级结构；同时设计一个会递归调用自身的构建求解器（construction solver）。每次求解调用遵循统一的完整流程：建立整体（establish the whole）→ 递归重建未解决的部分（recursively reconstruct unresolved parts）→ 回到整体以精炼它们的组合（revisit the whole to refine composition）。这种"global-local-global"递归让细尺度结构拥有自己的感知—编辑循环，同时保持场景级的几何与关系。关键技术点包括：(1) 参考对齐视图（reference-aligned views），在不同递归层级间传播共享的相机投影；(2) 父级回访（parent revisitation），用于处理局部精炼之后才出现的边界、空间关系与共享误差；(3) 一个视觉—语言编码智能体（vision-language coding agent），直接对比参考图像与场景渲染结果，用以驱动精炼、递归下降（recursive descent）与返回。

Result: 论文在复杂场景上评估，报告RCWM优于此前的基于代码的图像到场景重建方法。消融研究进一步支持递归构造带来的收益，并表明更深的递归调用可以改善更细尺度的重建效果。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、定量指标（如渲染/几何误差指标）或数值结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出RCWM框架及其"递归构造"原则，用RSP表示与自递归构建求解器把单张参考图像重建为复杂可执行3D世界，并通过参考对齐视图与父级回访处理跨层级的投影一致性与局部—全局误差。其意义在于为从视觉证据构建复杂可执行世界提供了一种通用的递归构造范式，并暗示递归深度可作为控制重建精细度的手段。潜在局限在于摘要未量化说明方法在极端复杂场景下的可扩展性、递归调用带来的计算/收敛代价，以及视觉—语言智能体对比渲染的可靠性与误差传播问题。

Abstract: Code world models represent worlds as executable programs, but this representation alone does not determine how to construct a complex world. We introduce Recursive Code World Models (RCWM), a framework for reconstructing complex 3D worlds in code from a single reference image. RCWM couples a Recursive Scene Program (RSP) representation with a construction solver that recursively calls itself. An RSP represents the executable world as compositional scene code, while each solver call follows the same complete process: establish the whole, recursively reconstruct unresolved parts, and revisit the whole to refine their composition. This global-local-global recursion gives fine-scale structures their own perception-and-editing loops while preserving scene-wide geometry and relationships. Reference-aligned views propagate a shared camera projection across levels, while parent revisitation addresses boundaries, spatial relations, and shared errors that emerge after local refinement. A vision-language coding agent directly compares reference images with scene renders to guide refinement, recursive descent, and return. Across complex scenes, RCWM outperforms prior code-based image-to-scene reconstruction methods. Ablation studies further support the benefits of recursive construction and suggest that deeper calls can improve finer-scale reconstruction. RCWM provides a recursive construction principle for building complex executable worlds from visual evidence.

</details>


### [53] [UBone3D: Physics-Rectified Conditional Flow Matching for Anatomical 3D Shape Completion from Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11506)
*Weiying Chen,Yuchong Gao,Siyuan Li,Marek Reformat,Rui Zheng,Edmond Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UBone3D 提出一个基于物理校正条件流匹配（physics-rectified conditional flow matching, CFM）的框架，可直接从部分且充满伪影的 3D 超声（US）点云中补全出干净、完整的解剖结构，并通过测试时物理校正联合引导生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 超声是一种安全、无辐射的成像模态，适合作为 CT 和 X 射线的补充手段用于纵向随访监测。然而，从超声分割得到的部分点云含有大量伪影（如表面增厚、条纹、信号丢失 dropout），使得从这类 US 点云中恢复干净且完整的解剖结构非常困难。现有方法未显式建模超声形成过程中确定性的物理伪影，导致补全结果在解剖保真度和物理一致性上存在不足，这促使作者提出显式物理建模与校正的方案。

Method: UBone3D 采用条件流匹配（CFM）直接对部分 US 观测进行点云补全，核心设计在于对物理伪影的显式建模与测试时校正：(1) 通过一个模拟的物理代理（simulated physics proxy）建模超声中确定性的物理伪影，如表面增厚、条纹和信号丢失；(2) 引入测试时物理校正（test-time physics rectification），在推理阶段对补全过程进行引导；(3) 补全由两个解耦的驱动力共同引导——其一是由在 CT 数据上训练的生成式形状先验 BoneFM 施加的解剖合理性约束，其二是由 USimNet 在超声成像形成空间中施加的物理一致性约束。

Result: 论文在模拟数据和活体（in-vivo）数据上进行了大量实验，结果显示 UBone3D 在重建精度和解剖保真度上相较于现有基线方法有显著提升。摘要未给出具体的数据集规模、评价指标名称以及量化数值。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出 UBone3D 框架，将物理校正引入条件流匹配，从而在超声点云补全任务中同时兼顾解剖合理性与物理一致性，并借助 CT 先验 BoneFM 与超声形成空间网络 USimNet 实现解耦的双重引导。其意义在于为放射安全的 3D 超声纵向监测提供更可靠的解剖结构恢复手段。可能的局限性在于摘要中未披露量化结果、数据集细节及计算开销，且方法依赖模拟物理代理与 CT 训练的生成先验，其泛化性与跨设备/跨域鲁棒性尚待验证。

Abstract: Three-dimensional ultrasound (US) is a safe, radiation-free complementary modality to CT and X-rays for longitudinal monitoring, yet its segmentation-derived partial point clouds are extremely artifact-laden. Consequently, it is challenging to recover a clean and complete anatomical structure from such US point clouds. In this paper, we present UBone3D, a novel framework based on physics-rectified conditional flow matching (CFM) that performs point cloud completion directly from partial US observations. UBone3D models deterministic physics artifacts (e.g., surface thickening, streaking, dropouts) via a simulated physics proxy, and introduces test-time physics rectification to steer the shape completion. At inference, the completion is jointly steered by two decoupled forces: (1) anatomical plausibility enforced by a CT-trained generative shape prior, BoneFM, and (2) physics consistency enforced by USimNet in the ultrasound formation space. Extensive experiments on simulated and in-vivo data demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and anatomical fidelity over existing baselines.

</details>


### [54] [Harnessing Intrinsic Subject-Aware Attention for Controllable Multi-Subject Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11507)
*Niange Yu,Ye Tian,Biaolong Chen,Miao Lu,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Yunhai Tong,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现 Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) 内部的某些注意力模块会自然形成"内在空间定位图"（Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map, ISGM），可精确锁定参考主体。基于此提出 DIAL 框架，在低噪声阶段用 ISGM 引导注意力以实现推理期可控的保真强度，在高噪声阶段用同一信号零成本构建偏好对进行强化学习，从而锚定注意力、缓解语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体视频生成面临两大难题：(1) 对参考主体的保真强度不可控（fidelity strength uncontrollable）；(2) 生成过程中存在语义漂移（semantic drift），即主体身份/属性随帧演化而偏离参考。现有方法通常依赖额外训练或外部条件，难以在推理阶段灵活调节保真程度，也缺乏廉价有效的机制抑制漂移。作者通过剖析 DiT 内部机制，试图从模型自身的注意力信号中找到统一解决方案，以提升身份一致性与可控性。

Method: 核心洞察：DiT 中特定的注意力块在去噪过程中自发形成 ISGM，能够精确 spatial grounding 参考主体，且无需额外监督即可获取。据此提出 DIAL（Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging），按扩散去噪噪声水平分两阶段利用该内部信号：(1) 低噪声阶段——利用 ISGM 引导注意力机制，在不重新训练的前提下实现对保真强度的精确控制；(2) 高噪声阶段——用同样的 ISGM 自动构建偏好对（preference pairs），零额外标注成本地驱动强化学习，将模型注意力锚定到参考主体上，抑制语义漂移。方法整体为训练与推理统一的框架，复用同一内部表征。

Result: 在 OpenS2V-Eval 基准上进行大量实验，DIAL 显著优于基线模型，在身份一致性（identity consistency）上持续提升，并实现了保真强度的可控调节。论文强调 RL 偏配对构建无需额外成本，且低噪声注意力引导无需重训练。

Conclusion: 贡献：(1) 揭示 DiT 注意力块自发形成可定位参考主体的 ISGM；(2) 提出 DIAL，将同一内部信号同时用于推理期可控保真与训练期 RL 偏好构建，统一解决保真不可控与语义漂移问题；(3) 在 OpenS2V-Eval 上验证有效性。意义在于为多主体视频生成提供了不依赖额外标注、可复数利用的内部机制。潜在局限：论文未给出具体定量指标、消融与计算开销分析；ISGM 的普适性（是否跨模型/架构稳定）与鲁棒性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Multi-subject video generation faces two key challenges: uncontrollable fidelity strength and potential semantic drift. We address these by analyzing the internal mechanisms of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). We found that certain attention blocks naturally form an Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM) that precisely locates reference subjects. Building on this insight, we propose Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging (DIAL), a framework that uses these internal signals for both training and inference. In low-noise stages, we use ISGM to guide the attention mechanism, allowing precise control over fidelity strength during inference without retraining. In high-noise stages, we use these same maps to automatically build preference pairs at no additional cost for Reinforcement Learning (RL). This RL procedure effectively anchors the model's attention to reference subjects and mitigates semantic drift. Extensive experiments show that DIAL significantly outperforms baseline models on the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, consistently improving identity consistency and enabling controllable fidelity strength.

</details>


### [55] [Prototype Matters: Modality-unified Prototype Self-distillation for Unsupervised Visible-infrared Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11514)
*Menglin Wang,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoSeD框架，通过模态统一原型对比（modality-unified prototypical contrast）与原型引导自蒸馏（prototype-guided self-distillation）联合优化可见光-红外行人重识别（VI-ReID）中的跨模态关联，替代传统最优传输的硬标签分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督可见光-红外行人重识别（VI-ReID）的核心挑战在于建立可靠的跨模态关联。最优传输（Optimal Transport）虽被证明是实用的跨模态关联方案，但存在两点不足：(1) 硬标签分配（hard label assignment）过于刚硬，未考虑聚类噪声的影响；(2) 仅施加跨模态对比（cross-modality contrast）是次优的，无法同时联合优化模态内与模态间的相似性关系。因此需要一个能同时建模模态内/模态间相似性、并对噪声更鲁棒的关联学习框架。

Method: 框架由两个统一优化的组件构成。其一为模态统一原型对比（modality-unified prototypical contrast）：不再与跨模态原型进行对比，而是用模态统一的原型同时联合优化模态内与模态间的相似性关系，从而更有效地促进模态不变性（modality invariance）。其二为原型引导自蒸馏（prototype-guided self-distillation）：以自原型（self-prototype）作为稳定的"教师"，通过师生式的在线自蒸馏来精炼实例-原型（instance-prototype）的在线关系（online relation），从而缓解硬标签分配带来的刚性和聚类噪声影响。两组件在统一框架中联合优化，模型结构简单但有效。

Result: 在标准VI-ReID基准上进行了大量对比与分析实验，验证了所提方法的有效性；论文提供了开源代码（https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：提出模态统一原型对比以联合优化模态内/模态间相似性、引入原型引导自蒸馏以稳定并精炼实例-原型关系，并将二者统一为简单有效的无监督VI-ReID框架，在标准基准上取得验证。局限性与具体指标未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: Estimating reliable cross-modality association is crucial to unsupervised visible-infrared person re-ID. While optimal transport is shown to be a practical solution for cross-modality association, it suffers from the rigidness of hard label assignment without considering the impact of cluster noise. Moreover, enforcing only cross-modality contrast is also suboptimal, as it fails to jointly optimize the similarity relation within and across modality. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for cross-modality learning by well exploitation of prototypes: First, instead of contrasting with cross-modality prototypes, we show that modality-unified prototypical contrast facilitates better modality invariance by jointly and simultaneously optimizing similarity relation within and across-modality. Taking self-prototype as a steady teacher, we further refine the instance-prototype online relation through prototype-guided self-distillation. The two components are optimized in a unified framework, leading to a simple yet effective model. On standard VI-ReID benchmarks, we perform extensive comparison and analysis, validating the effectiveness of our proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD.

</details>


### [56] [LoopVAE: Recurrent Depth Across Scales for Visual Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11516)
*Zhiying Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopVAE 提出将分层视觉 tokenizer 中跨尺度、跨循环的计算核心参数共享，用一个循环条件化的共享核心替代各尺度独立模块，在约 29M 参数下于 ImageNet-256 达到 0.28 rFID 与 32.54 dB PSNR，参数比 84M 参考 VAE 少约 65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层视觉 tokenizer 通常为不同空间尺度分配不同的处理模块（参数不共享），导致参数量随尺度数量线性增长。作者探究一个关键问题：这类计算中有多少可以复用同一组参数。减少参数对高效、可部署的视觉生成/重建模型（如边缘与实时系统）具有实际意义，同时循环深度本身也可能带来表示能力的收益。

Method: LoopVAE 在尺度内部与尺度之间复用一个「尺度条件 + 循环条件」的核心模块，而仅让改变分辨率的过渡层保持独立。核心由四个 block 组成，编解码器各执行 28 次 block 应用（即循环复用形成等效深度）。作者设计了两类算子变体：卷积分支与 Transformer 分支，并支持单分辨率与多分辨率 latent 接口，从而在同一执行图下对比参数共享方案。实验还包含针对循环的干预分析（补全训练时的递归、施加微小特征更新、截断递归）来检验循环计算的作用。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，29M 参数的卷积模型在约 30 epoch 的两阶段训练预算下取得 0.28 rFID 与 32.54 dB PSNR，参数量比 84M 参考 VAE 少约 65%。非对抗 Transformer 消融在相同执行图与全局共享下获得有竞争力的 PSNR/SSIM，但非共享 block 在 LPIPS 上更优。循环干预显示：完成已训练的递归可提升重建质量，且即使很小的特征更新也会产生显著的下游影响；截断递归会暴露输出范围误差，可用于区分「有用的循环计算」与「可靠的提前退出」。运行时分析表明参数共享存在执行权衡：存储权重更少，但算术量更大、运行时间更长。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是把「跨尺度的循环深度」确立为视觉 tokenization 的参数共享设计轴，验证了在卷积与 Transformer 算子、单/多分辨率 latent 接口下共享核心的可行性，并给出参数—精度—运行时之间的权衡证据。局限包括：共享核心带来更高的算术开销与更长运行时间；非对抗 Transformer 共享版本在 LPIPS 上不如非共享版本；结果主要基于 ImageNet-256 与受限训练预算。

Abstract: Hierarchical visual tokenizers typically allocate different processing blocks to different spatial scales. We ask how much of this computation can use the same parameters. LoopVAE reuses a scale- and loop-conditioned core within and across scales, while keeping resolution-changing transitions independent. A four-block core executes 28 block applications per encoder or decoder. On ImageNet-256, the 29M-parameter convolutional model reaches 0.28 rFID and 32.54 dB PSNR under an approximately 30-epoch two-stage training budget, using approximately 65% fewer parameters than the 84M reference VAEs. A non-adversarial Transformer ablation with the same execution graph finds competitive PSNR and SSIM under global sharing, although unshared blocks improve LPIPS. Targeted loop interventions show that completing the trained recurrence improves reconstruction and that even small feature updates can have substantial downstream effects. Truncation also exposes output-range errors, distinguishing useful recurrent computation from reliable early exit. Runtime profiling reveals the execution tradeoff: fewer stored weights require more arithmetic and longer runtime in the tested configurations. With convolutional and Transformer operators and single- or multi-resolution latent interfaces, LoopVAE establishes recurrent depth across scales as a parameter-sharing design axis for visual tokenization.

</details>


### [57] [Learning Interaction between Image and Layout Priors for Joint Image-Layout Generation in Design Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11519)
*Shirong Yang,Bo Yang,Ying Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InterIL，一个将背景图像与前景布局在单一生成过程中联合建模的扩散模型，通过可学习的通信模块连接预训练的图像与布局扩散骨干网络，显式建模图像-布局的双向交互，从而更好地捕捉联合图像-布局分布并提升版面和谐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务是图形设计模板生成：给定输入文本，需同时生成背景图像与前景元素布局，形成和谐构图。现有工作多采用串行（sequential）范式，先/后依次生成各设计元素，作者认为这种串行方案难以忠实刻画背景与布局之间的依赖关系，因而无法准确建模联合图像-布局分布，限制了生成模板的质量与构图和谐度。此外，许多方法引入设计专用归纳偏置，可能偏离真实设计数据的原始特性。

Method: 核心方法为 InterIL：在单次生成过程中联合生成背景图像与布局两种模态。技术要点包括：(1) 复用预训练的图像扩散模型与布局扩散模型作为骨干，并通过一个可学习的通信模块（communication module）将二者连接，显式建模图像-布局之间的双向交互；(2) 训练时冻结图像与布局骨干，仅更新通信模块，从而保留并利用大规模预训练的单模态先验，使模型专注于学习跨模态交互、更好地逼近联合分布；(3) 模型不含设计专用归纳偏置，以保留真实设计本身的特征；(4) 引入测试时引导（test-time guidance）策略，使用户在推理阶段施加特定偏好而无需重新训练。

Result: 论文通过实验与先前方法对比，报告其模型在三个维度上取得明显更好的结果：图像质量、布局质量以及图像-布局的和谐一致性，生成样本更接近真实样本。此外，作者展示了模型在推理阶段灵活施加用户偏好而无需再训练的能力。（摘要未给出具体数据集、评测指标与数值结果。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出联合建模图像与布局的 InterIL 框架，用通信模块桥接冻结的预训练单模态扩散骨干，显式学习跨模态交互以捕捉联合图像-布局分布，并支持测试时偏好引导。其意义在于摒弃串行生成范式与设计专用归纳偏置，提升版面设计的协调性并增强可控性。局限在于：摘要未披露定量评测细节与数据集，冻结骨干可能限制对特定设计域分布的适应能力，且通信模块的容量与联合生成的推理开销有待评估。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of graphic design template creation, which generates a background image and a layout of foreground elements over the background to form a harmonious composition from an input text.
  Prior work on graphic design generation mostly adopts a sequential paradigm, where design elements are generated sequentially. We argue that such a sequential scheme falls short of faithfully capturing the dependency between the background and layout (and thus the joint image-layout distribution), which limits the quality of generated design templates.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a model, InterIL, which jointly generates the two modalities, background image and layout, in a single generative process. The novel design of our joint model connects the backbones of pretrained image and layout diffusion models with a learnable communication module to explicitly model bidirectional image-layout interaction. During training, the image and layout backbones are frozen to maintain and leverage the vast pretrained single-modality prior knowledge, while only the communication module is updated, so that the model can focus on learning image-layout interaction and thereby better capture the joint image-layout distribution for improved composition harmony.
  Our model has no design-specific inductive bias, which allows it to better preserve the original characteristics of realistic designs. We further introduce a test-time guidance strategy to enable users to impose their specific preferences on generated results.
  Our experiments show that, compared with prior approaches, our model can generate significantly better results in terms of image, layout and image-layout harmonization, producing outputs closer to real samples.
