We also demonstrate the flexibility of our model in enforcing user preferences at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [58] [World in World: Explore the World with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11548)
*Chenxi Song,Yanming Yang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World in World，一种无需训练的推理时接口，将多种异构控制证据（源视频观测、目标视角投影、几何渲染、缓存外检索状态）转化为带相机与时间标签的干净视觉状态，并通过冻结因果视频世界模型的原生自注意力读取，实现相机可控重渲染、长时程回访与人体动作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频世界模型支持交互式长时程探索，但对源视频进行新视角探索时存在多重耦合需求：生成结果需与原始事件保持时序同步、将观测内容放置到目标视角、合理补全新暴露区域，并在回访时恢复此前生成的外观。现有方法通常依赖任务专用模块或额外训练，控制灵活性差、泛化成本高。因此需要一种无需训练、可统一支持多种控制证据的推理时接口。

Method: 核心是一个无需训练（training-free）的推理时接口，把异构控制证据统一编码为相机与时间标注的干净视觉状态，并以 token 级形式注入冻结的因果视频模型自注意力中。证据来源包括：源视频观测、目标视角场景投影、用于引导新暴露主体区域补全的几何渲染，以及滚动缓存之外检索到的已生成状态；每个证据源都携带 token 级支持度与各自的可用性调度。对应关系路由器（correspondence router）结合持久点身份与几何信息建立 token 对应，把受支持的查询导向匹配的源视频 token。证据级注意力 CFG（EWA）在同一次去噪前向传播中，依据注意力响应独立调节各辅助通道的额外贡献。整套接口共享同一冻结主干。

Result: 论文在相机可控的视频重渲染任务上评估 World in World，涵盖多样化的视角变化，考察感知质量、时序一致性与相机跟随精度。同一接口在冻结主干下同时支持相机控制重渲染、长时程回访与人体动作迁移，说明无需为不同任务单独训练即可复用（摘要未给出具体数据集名称与量化数值）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一个统一的、免训练的推理时控制接口，将多种异构控制证据以 token 对应与可调注意力贡献的形式接入冻结因果视频世界模型，从而在不额外训练的前提下实现相机控制、长时程回访与动作迁移。其意义在于提升世界模型的可控性与复用性。潜在局限在于性能依赖冻结主干的原生自注意力与外部几何/检索证据的质量，且摘要未报告定量指标，通用性与鲁棒性仍需更多验证。

Abstract: Autoregressive video world models enable interactive, long-horizon exploration, but flexible control remains challenging. Exploring a source video from new viewpoints requires the generated rollout to remain synchronised with the recorded event, place observed content in the requested view, plausibly complete newly exposed regions, and recover previously generated appearance on revisits. Existing methods typically address these requirements through task-specific modules or additional training. We present World in World, a training-free inference-time interface that converts heterogeneous control evidence into camera- and time-labelled clean visual states, which are read through the native self attention of a frozen causal video model. The evidence comprises source-video observations, target-view scene projections, geometry renderings that guide completion of newly exposed subject regions, and retrieved generated states beyond the rolling cache. Each evidence source carries token-level support and its own availability schedule. A correspondence router combines persistent point identities with geometry to establish token correspondences, guiding supported queries towards matching source-video tokens. Evidence-wise attention CFG (EWA) then independently regulates each auxiliary channel's additional contribution using attention responses from the same denoising forward pass. The shared interface supports camera-controlled rerendering, long-horizon revisiting, and human-motion transfer with the same frozen backbone. We evaluate World in World on camera-controlled video rerendering under diverse viewpoint changes, assessing perceptual quality, temporal consistency, and camera-following accuracy.

</details>


### [59] [A Comparative Evaluation of Pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks for Melanoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11550)
*Wagner Moreno Schmitz,Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa,Thiago Magalhães Amaral,Dalcimar Casanova,Jefferson Tales Oliva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在统一的训练协议下，系统性地评估了多种预训练卷积神经网络（ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet、InceptionV3）在皮肤病变分类任务中的表现，并比较其在皮肤镜图像与组织病理学图像两种成像模态上的性能差异，发现ResNet50在两种模态下均表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素瘤的早期诊断对提高患者生存率至关重要，但黑色素瘤与其他皮肤病变之间存在高度视觉相似性，加之图像采集条件多变，使得临床精准区分病变类型面临巨大挑战。人工智能尤其是CNN因其强大的层次化特征提取能力，被广泛用于皮肤镜与组织病理学图像的自动分析。然而，尽管已有大量预训练CNN架构被提出，针对特定成像模态如何选择最合适的网络仍是一个尚未解决的开放性问题，缺乏在统一训练协议下的横向对比研究。

Method: 研究采用迁移学习范式，选用五种预训练CNN架构——ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet和InceptionV3，在相同训练协议下进行皮肤病变分类。方法核心在于控制变量，即固定训练流程、超参数与数据处理方式，仅改变网络架构与成像模态，以隔离架构本身对性能的影响。评估覆盖皮肤镜图像（HAM10000、ISIC 2018）与组织病理学图像（CR-AI4SkIN）两类数据模态，从而对比同一架构在不同成像模态下的表现差异。

Result: 实验在HAM10000、ISIC 2018和CR-AI4SkIN三个数据集上进行。在皮肤镜图像上，模型准确率介于71%（InceptionV3，ISIC 2018）至84%（ResNet50，HAM10000）之间；在组织病理学图像（CR-AI4SkIN）上，准确率介于72%（VGG19）至83%（ResNet50）之间。结果显示：不同成像模态下模型性能存在差异；在皮肤镜图像上表现相近的架构，在组织病理学数据上却表现出不同的性能，说明架构选择需与成像模态相匹配。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提供了一个在统一训练协议下跨成像模态的预训练CNN系统性基准对比，明确了ResNet50在皮肤镜与组织病理学图像上均取得最佳准确率，并揭示架构性能对成像模态的依赖性。其意义在于为皮肤病变分类任务中的网络选型提供了实证依据。局限性包括：仅评估分类准确率，未报告敏感性、特异性、AUC等重要临床指标；模型性能受数据集与预训练权重影响，泛化性有待验证；未涉及更先进架构或针对类别不平衡、病变细粒度区分等难点的专门处理。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of melanoma is critical for improving patient survival rates. However, accurately distinguishing melanoma from other skin lesions remains a significant clinical challenge due to the high visual similarity among lesion types and variability in image acquisition conditions. Artificial intelligence, particularly machine learning, has emerged as a promising tool to support dermatological diagnosis by automating feature extraction from medical images. Among the available approaches, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong performance in image classification tasks, making them well-suited for analyzing both dermatoscopic and histopathological images, given their ability to capture hierarchical visual patterns relevant to lesion characterization. Nevertheless, despite numerous pre-trained CNN architectures having been proposed, selecting the most appropriate one for a given imaging modality remains an open challenge. In this study, we evaluate pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for skin lesion classification using dermatoscopic and histopathological image datasets. Experiments were conducted on the HAM10000, ISIC 2018, and CR-AI4SkIN datasets, evaluating the ResNet50, VGG16, VGG19, MobileNet, and InceptionV3 architectures under the same training protocol. The experimental evaluation showed that the models achieved accuracies ranging from 71% (InceptionV3 on ISIC 2018) to 84% (ResNet50 on HAM10000) on dermatoscopic images. For histopathological images, accuracies ranged from 72% (VGG19) to 83% (ResNet50) on the CR-AI4SkIN dataset. The results demonstrate that model performance differs between dermatoscopic and histopathological image modalities, showing that architectures exhibiting similar performance on dermatoscopic images exhibit different performance on histopathological data.

</details>


### [60] [Learn the Solid, Not the File: Canonical Inputs for Neural Networks on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11573)
*Heinrich Jiang,Hager Yasser Mohamed,Alexander Hitt,Valeriia Lomakina,Henning Jiang,Jennifer Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"规范区域图"（canonical region graph），一种从实体本身推导节点、特征与坐标系的B-rep输入表示，具备对表面重划分（repartitioning）与刚体运动的可证明不变性，在标准基准上匹配最强基线，并在所有扰动下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-rep是CAD系统表达参数化三维模型的标准格式，但同一实体可由多种不同B-rep表示：不同建模操作、几何内核重建文件、导出设置重新划分面等都会产生差异。论文指出，现有B-rep编码器对这种"同一实体、不同表示"的变化不鲁棒——在标准基准扰动、CAD软件固有变异以及设计者建模差异下性能常常灾难性崩溃。这一鲁棒性问题对依赖B-rep的CAD下游任务（检索、分类、分割等）至关重要，因为表示形式的变化不应改变模型的语义识别结果。

Method: 核心创新是规范区域图表示。与依赖操作历史或B-rep表面划分方式的传统编码不同，其节点、节点特征与坐标参考系均直接从实体几何本身推导，而非从表面分片结构推导。论文给出了该表示关于表面重划分和刚体运动的理论不变性保证，即同一实体的不同B-rep以及经过刚性变换的模型会映射到一致的、等价的图结构。这种不变性从表示层面而非模型训练层面解决问题。此外，作者构建了一个由多名设计者用FreeCAD对同一零件建模的人类数据集，用于评估同一零件不同建模方式的表示差异。

Result: 实验在标准B-rep基准、人工施加的扰动、CAD软件固有的自然变异，以及新构建的FreeCAD人类数据集上进行。评估对象为流行的B-rep编码器。结果显示：现有编码器在各类扰动下性能往往灾难性下降，而规范区域图在标准基准上达到与最强基线相当的性能，并在论文测试的每一种扰动下都保持稳定。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了B-rep编码普遍存在的表示鲁棒性缺陷，并从表示设计角度提出规范区域图这一根本性解决方案，具备理论不变性保证与实证稳定性，同时贡献了一个人类建模数据集。意义在于将B-rep学习的鲁棒性问题从模型端转移到输入表示端。可能局限：摘要未说明对噪声、孔洞、非流形或几何精度近似等更复杂畸变的鲁棒性，以及规范化的计算开销与对下游任务（如分割、检索）的泛化效果。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-rep) is the standard format used by modern CAD systems for parametric 3D models. It turns out, the exact same solid can be represented by different B-reps: for example, two engineers using different operations, a geometry kernel rebuilding the file, and an export setting repartitioning faces will lead to different B-reps even though the underlying solid remains the same.
  We show that existing B-rep encoders are not robust to variation in the B-rep with the same solid on perturbations applied to standard benchmarks, naturally occurring variations inherent to CAD software, and differences in how designers model the same part via a human dataset we created in FreeCAD. The performance of popular B-rep encoders often collapses catastrophically.
  We propose the canonical region graph, an input representation whose nodes, features and coordinate frame are derived from the solid itself and show theoretical invariance guarantees on repartitioning and rigid motions. It matches the strongest baseline on standard benchmarks, and is stable under every perturbation we test.

</details>


### [61] [OmniKVQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11582)
*Suho Yoo,Hyunjong Ok,Jongmin Choi,Jihoo Jung,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OmniKVQuant——一个无需训练的 Omni-LLM KV cache 量化框架，针对多模态缓存中"时序键漂移"与"异构值几何"两大问题，分别采用短窗口键量化范围与逐模态值旋转，在 Qwen2.5-Omni / Qwen3-Omni 上实现 2-bit KV cache 且基本保持性能，并提供融合 Triton 解码内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-modal LLM 需同时处理音频、视频与文本，KV cache 显存开销随多模态长输入急剧增长，成为推理部署的关键瓶颈。文本 LLM 中 KV cache 量化已是成熟方案，但将其直接迁移到 Omni-LLM 尚未被系统研究。作者发现代表性旋转式量化方法（TurboQuant）在多模态缓存上存在两个问题：(1) 时序键漂移（temporal key drift）——不同时间窗口的 key 分布差异导致全局量化范围失配；(2) 异构值几何（heterogeneous value geometry）——不同模态的 value 分布几何特性不一致，单一共享旋转无法兼顾。该问题对音视频实时推理与边缘部署至关重要。

Method: OmniKVQuant 是一个 training-free（免训练）的推理期框架：（i）键（key）量化：不做全局范围估计，而是在输入流的每个短时间窗口内单独设定量化范围，以缓解时序键漂移；（ii）值（value）量化：对每个模态分别进行旋转（per-modality rotation），使不同模态的 value 几何被各自对齐，而非共用同一旋转矩阵。此外提供融合 Triton 解码内核，在 attention 计算过程中即时解包 2-bit 缓存，避免构建稠密 FP16 KV cache，从而降低显存并保持效率。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Omni 与 Qwen3-Omni 两个背骨模型上进行验证，覆盖七个音视频（audio-visual）基准。实验表明，OmniKVQuant 可将 KV cache 压至 2-bit，同时大幅保持原性能（substantially preserving performance），优于直接将 TurboQuant 应用于多模态缓存的基线。作者还实现并验证了融合 Triton 解码内核，做到不构建稠密 FP16 缓存。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：首次系统分析旋转式 KV cache 量化在 Omni-LLM 多模态缓存上的失效模式，提出时序窗口化 key 范围与逐模态 value 旋转的免训练方案，并配套 2-bit 融合 Triton 内核，在两大 Omni 模型与七项音视频基准上实现约 2-bit 的高压缩且性能损失可控。意义在于低比特 KV 量化对音视频多模态实时/边缘推理的显存优化具有实用价值。局限：方法依赖旋转式量化假设，未给出量化到更低比特（如 1-bit）的评估；实验仅限两种 Qwen-Omni 架构，泛化性有待验证；未涉及 event-based/SNN 等神经形态感知场景。

Abstract: As Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) take in audio, video and text together, their KV cache memory cost grows. KV cache quantization is the de facto approach in text-only LLMs, but its application to Omni-LLMs remains unexplored. In this paper, we analyze how TurboQuant, a representative rotation-based KV cache quantization method, behaves on multimodal caches and identify two critical issues: temporal key drift and heterogeneous value geometry. To address these, we propose OmniKVQuant, a training-free framework that (i) sets the key quantization range over each short window of the input stream; and (ii) rotates values separately per modality. On Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni, OmniKVQuant enables 2-bit KV caches while substantially preserving performance across seven audio-visual benchmarks. We further provide a fused Triton decode kernel that unpacks the 2-bit cache during attention, so no dense FP16 cache is ever built. Code: https://github.com/kaistmm/OmniKVQuant

</details>


### [62] [MMGait: Benchmarking and Unifying Gait Recognition across Heterogeneous Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11601)
*Saihui Hou,Chenye Wang,Qingyuan Cai,Aoqi Li,Yongzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MMGait大规模多传感器步态识别基准（融合可见光、红外、深度、LiDAR、雷达五种模态），并在此基础上提出"全模态步态识别"（Omni-Modal Gait Recognition）任务与统一模型OmniGait++，用单一联合训练权重覆盖单模态、跨模态与多模态识别设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流步态识别多依赖RGB视频及其派生的轮廓、骨架，但人类行走实际产生的是异质的光度、几何与运动线索，仅靠RGB难以系统研究。现有基准无法同时比较多种传感模态，且每新增一种模态或模态组合，就需要单独训练一个专家模型，导致严重的可扩展性问题。因此作者希望建立统一的多传感器测试平台，并探索在模态可用性动态变化时统一识别身份的可行性。

Method: 1) 构建MMGait：将可见光、红外、深度、LiDAR、雷达观测对齐到序列级（sequence-level）对应，覆盖外观、轮廓、几何、运动和身体结构等多样线索，并采用统一的impostor-augmented评测协议。2) 提出Omni-Modal Gait Recognition任务，在共享身份空间中统一单模态、跨模态（有向检索）与多模态识别。3) OmniGait++架构：各模态使用模态专属前端（modality-specific front ends）提取模态相关线索，再接入共享身份编码器（shared identity encoder）学习可比较的身份描述子；锚点引导融合模块（anchor-guided fusion）可在无帧级同步条件下聚合不同规模、不同组成与基数的模态子集。4) 单一联合训练checkpoint即可覆盖全部三种识别设置。

Result: 在MMGait基准的共享impostor-augmented协议下进行实验，涵盖单模态识别、跨模态有向检索和多模态识别（task-specific experts）。结果显示：模态排名随probe条件变化而变化，跨模态对齐仍具挑战性，融合常带来互补增益。OmniGait++在多数共享设置下与任务专属专家模型保持竞争力，并能扩展到固定配对模型无法支持的高基数融合场景，验证了统一识别在不同模态可用性下的可行性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：发布多传感器步态基准MMGait作为异质步态感知的通用测试平台；提出Omni-Modal Gait Recognition任务；设计OmniGait++统一模型以单权重覆盖单/跨/多模态识别并支持变基数融合。该工作推动了多模态感知与统一表征学习在生物特征识别中的应用。潜在局限：论文聚焦步态识别，未涉及事件相机等神经形态传感模态；"在多数设置下保持竞争力"暗示相比专用专家可能仍有性能折损；跨模态对齐困难也表明统一身份空间的判别性仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Gait recognition is commonly studied using RGB videos or their derived silhouettes and poses. Yet human walking produces heterogeneous photometric, geometric, and motion cues that cannot be systematically examined with RGB-centered benchmarks. We present MMGait, a large-scale multi-sensor benchmark that brings visible, infrared, depth, LiDAR, and radar observations into sequence-level correspondence. It provides diverse modalities spanning appearance, contours, geometry, motion, and body structure. Under a shared impostor-augmented protocol, we evaluate single-modal recognition, cross-modal recognition via directed retrieval, and multi-modal recognition using task-specific experts. Across settings, modality rankings vary with probe conditions, cross-modal alignment remains difficult, and fusion often provides complementary gains. This analysis exposes a scalability problem: individual modalities, modality pairs, and fusion configurations are typically handled by separately trained experts. We formulate Omni-Modal Gait Recognition, which unifies single-modal, cross-modal, and multi-modal recognition within a shared identity space. OmniGait++ uses modality-specific front ends followed by a shared identity encoder to preserve modality-dependent cues while learning comparable identity descriptors. An anchor-guided fusion module aggregates modality subsets of varying size without frame-level synchronization. A jointly trained checkpoint covers all three recognition settings and accommodates modality subsets of different compositions and cardinalities. Experiments show OmniGait++ remains competitive with task-specific experts in many shared settings and extends to higher-cardinality fusion unavailable to fixed-pair models. The results establish MMGait as a common testbed for heterogeneous gait sensing and demonstrate the feasibility of unified recognition under varying modality availability.

</details>


### [63] [LangStreet: Persistent Language Fields for Anchor-Decoded Street Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11616)
*Runyi Yang,Deheng Zhang,Xiaoye Wang,Mengjiao Ma,Lei Sun,Kanzhi Wu,Ajad Chhatkuli,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对"锚点解码式"结构化高斯场景（anchor-decoded Gaussian scenes），提出一种持久化语言场（persistent language field）。核心思想是"语义所有权"（semantic ownership）：瞬态子高斯只负责路由观测，而语言场由持久化解码器槽位及其父锚点拥有。通过alpha合成责任度累积方向性证据并在槽位与锚点间守恒，再以锚点相对语义坐标下的低秩残差表达细节，从而在几乎不损失精度的情况下将语义特征存储量降低约4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言高斯场（Language Gaussian fields）通常隐含一个假设：承载语义的基元在跨视角下保持可识别。但在可扩展的锚点解码表示中，该假设失效——持久化锚点会生成随视角变化的子高斯（view-conditioned child Gaussians），其几何与外观随相机变化，导致语义归属不明确、语义特征随视角漂移。这一问题在自动驾驶类大场景（KITTI、Waymo）中尤为突出，因为需要同时兼顾跨视角语义一致性与存储/计算可扩展性。作者因此提出需要一种"持久语义所有权 + 证据守恒"的表示框架。

Method: 关键设计有三点：(1) 语义所有权分离——瞬态子高斯仅用于路由观测，语言场的所有权归持久化解码器槽位（decoder slots）与其父锚点（parent anchors）所有，避免语义随视角变动。(2) 证据累积与守恒——利用alpha合成责任度（alpha-compositing responsibilities）在槽位上做加性方向性证据累积，这些统计量可精确边缘化（marginalize exactly）到锚点上，保证父子层级间的语义证据守恒；对弱支撑槽位使用锚点对齐证据补全，同时保持锚点方向不变。(3) 语义坐标与低秩残差——槽位细节以锚点相对语义坐标系（anchor-relative semantic coordinates）中的低秩残差表示，实现紧凑存储。模型提供三个变体：Ours (base) 存储锚点特征加紧凑槽位残差；Ours (light) 仅保留锚点特征；Ours (max) 显式存储完整维度的补全槽位特征。整个过程无需针对场景做特定语义优化。

Result: 在KITTI、Virtual KITTI与Waymo三个自动驾驶数据集上评测，指标为2D mIoU与有效特征存储占用（effective feature footprint）。在KITTI上，Ours (base) 取得34.19 2D mIoU，有效特征占用仅2.72 GiB；而Ours (max) 为34.20 mIoU、12.90 GiB，即精度几乎持平（差0.01 mIoU）但存储降低约4.7倍。相同的"精度—存储"趋势在Virtual KITTI与Waymo上得到验证。结果表明，在视角条件化splat上的语言场需要满足三点：持久化语义所有权、证据守恒、以及兼顾稳定性/细节/表示代价的层级结构。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：指出锚点解码高斯表示中"跨视角语义可识别性"假设的失效问题，提出以持久化所有权与证据守恒为核心的语言场框架，并通过低秩残差与层级化槽位设计实现精度与存储的较优权衡，且无需场景特定的语义优化。其意义在于为可扩展自动驾驶场景的3D语言场提供了更可扩展的表示范式。潜在局限：评测集中在驾驶类数据集与2D mIoU指标，对更开放场景、开放词汇查询与3D语义评测的泛化性尚待验证；代码、checkpoint与benchmark将公开。

Abstract: Language Gaussian fields implicitly assume that the primitive carrying semantics remains identifiable across views. This assumption breaks in scalable anchor-decoded representations, where persistent anchors generate view-conditioned child Gaussians whose geometry and appearance vary with the camera. We introduce Ours, a persistent language field for such structured Gaussian scenes. Our key idea is semantic ownership: transient children route observations, while persistent decoder slots and their parent anchors own the language field. We use alpha-compositing responsibilities to accumulate additive directional evidence at slots; these statistics marginalize exactly to anchors. We then complete weakly supported slots with anchor-aligned evidence while preserving the anchor direction, and represent slot detail through low-rank residuals in anchor-relative semantic coordinates. Our primary model, Ours (base), stores anchor features together with compact slot residuals. Ours (light) retains only anchor features, whereas Ours (max) stores the full-dimensional completed slot features explicitly. Without scene-specific semantic optimization, Ours (base) nearly matches Ours (max) across KITTI, Virtual KITTI, and Waymo. On KITTI, it achieves 34.19 2D mIoU with a 2.72 GiB effective feature footprint, compared with 34.20 mIoU and 12.90 GiB for Ours (max). The same accuracy-storage trend holds on Virtual KITTI and Waymo. These results show that language fields on view-conditioned splats require persistent semantic ownership, conserved evidence, and a hierarchy that balances stability, detail, and representation cost. Our code, checkpoints, and benchmark suite will be publicly available.

</details>


### [64] [Vidu S2: Real-Time Interactive, Editable, and Spatial Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11638)
*Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Xu Wang,Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Dechuang Chen,Ming Lin,Jingjiang Zhou,Haopeng Jin,Qi Jia,Xiaohang Wang,Yaole Wang,Zhanqiang Zhang,Ran Li,Zhengkun Huang,Shuyue Xiong,Yuji Wang,Zikun Dai,Hui He,Yang Luo,Mang Ning,Weiqi Feng,Chengyang Ye,Xinyue Lin,Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Hengkai Tan,Zeyuan Wang,Chendong Xiang,Kaiwen Zheng,Zhijie Deng,Fan Bao,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vidu S2 是一套实时交互式视频生成系统，包含数字人模型 Vidu S2-Avatar（支持实时 720p 生成、动态参考更新与更强指令跟随）和实时视频编辑模型 Vidu S2-Editing（支持风格渲染、换衣、换角与背景替换），并进一步探索了二者的实时空间视频生成可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对实时交互式数字角色生成与视频流实时编辑的需求。相比前代 Vidu S1，作者希望突破分辨率、参考动态性和指令可控性方面的限制，使数字人能够支持随时更新的动态参考并执行如跳舞等更复杂指令；同时使视频编辑从离线扩展到流式实时场景，覆盖风格、服装、人物与背景等编辑任务。此外，作者还探索实时空间视频生成，以拓展交互式视频的沉浸式应用空间。

Method: 论文提出 Vidu S2 系统，由 Vidu S2-Avatar（实时交互式数字角色模型）与 Vidu S2-Editing（实时视频编辑模型）两个组件构成。Vidu S2-Avatar 相较 Vidu S1 支持实时 720p 视频生成、可在任意时刻更新的动态参考，以及更强的指令跟随能力（如舞蹈动作）。Vidu S2-Editing 面向视频流的实时编辑，支持风格渲染、服装替换、角色替换和背景替换。作者还进一步探索了面向二者的实时空间视频生成可行性。摘要未披露具体网络架构、训练策略、时序建模方式或推理加速细节，相关技术方案需参考正文。

Result: 实验表明 Vidu S2 在性能上优于所有基线方法。作者提供了可交互的在线演示（https://vidu.com/vidu-stream）。摘要未给出具体数据集、评估指标、分辨率/帧率数值或消融实验等定量结果，详细实验设置与量化对比需查阅原文。

Conclusion: Vidu S2 的主要贡献在于：将交互式数字人生成提升至实时 720p 水平，引入可随时更新的动态参考与更强的指令跟随；同时将视频编辑扩展到实时视频流场景，并探索了实时空间视频生成。其意义在于推动交互式视频生成从离线走向实时流式应用。潜在局限在于摘要层面缺少架构与实现细节、缺乏定量结果与评价指标，且实时空间视频生成仅停留在可行性探索阶段，其质量、延迟与一致性尚需正文进一步验证。

Abstract: We present Vidu S2, which comprises Vidu S2-Avatar, a real-time interactive digital-character model, and Vidu S2-Editing, a real-time video editing model. Moreover, we explore the feasibility of real-time spatial video generation for both Vidu S2-Avatar and Vidu S2-Editing. Compared with Vidu S1, Vidu S2-Avatar supports real-time 720p video generation, generation with dynamic references that can be updated at any moment, and stronger instruction following, such as dancing. Vidu S2-Editing supports editing a video stream in real time, including style rendering, clothing replacement, character replacement, and background replacement. Experiments show that Vidu S2 outperforms all baselines. A playable online demo is available at https://vidu.com/vidu-stream.

</details>


### [65] [Self-Supervised Cardiac Phase Detection via Single-Parameter Latent Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11650)
*John Bonnici,Matthew Baugh,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Sarah Cechnicka,Bernhard Kainz,Alberto Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"单参数潜在轨道"(latent orbit) 归纳偏置，将心动周期相位视为一维信号，把运动隐变量约束到由有界标量相位索引的潜在空间全局线性轨迹上，经正弦非线性映射得到具有一致时间序的振荡运动信号，从而无需标注即可直接定位 ED（舒张末期）与 ES（收缩末期）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图中 ED/ES 关键帧的准确定位是心室功能量化的基础，但人工挑选主观性强，导致显著的观察者间差异。现有自监督方法分两类：一类预设严格周期性轨迹，可解释性强但对心动时相演化施加过强假设；另一类通过重建或配准目标学习无约束的低维运动子空间，相位信息隐含在表示中，必须依赖后处理几何运算从学习轨迹中反推 ED/ES，缺乏直接性与可解释性。作者希望用生理学先验替代这些不自然的约束或后处理。

Method: 将"心动相位是一维信号"的生理观察转化为结构先验：把运动隐分量限制为潜在空间中的全局线性轨迹（潜在轨道），由单一有界标量相位变量索引；再通过正弦非线性映射该相位变量，得到振荡形式的运动信号。由于相位标量具有单调、一致的时间排序，ED/ES 可直接由学习到的相位信号识别，无需后处理几何反演。该轻量单参数相位模型保留捕捉不规则心律（如心律失常）的灵活性，在 EchoNet-Dynamic 上以无标注方式训练。

Result: 在 EchoNet-Dynamic 数据集上无标注训练；与先前自监督状态方法相比，ED 定位精度显著提升，ES 定位性能相当，同时使用更受约束的表示且所需训练轮次更少。实验表明合理的生理归纳偏置可在性能上匹配甚至超越更复杂的表示。代码已开源（https://github.com/BonniciJ/OrbitalEcho/）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出基于单参数潜在轨道的可解释心动相位表示，用生理先验替代严格周期假设或事后几何处理，实现了自监督 ED/ES 直接定位，并在更少训练轮次下取得更优或相当性能。不足之处：方法依赖"相位为一维振荡信号"的假设，应对高度不规则心律的鲁棒性有待验证；评估仅局限于单一数据集 EchoNet-Dynamic，未见多中心或跨设备泛化结果；文中未报告完整定量指标细节。

Abstract: Accurate identification of end-diastole (ED) and end-systole (ES) in echocardiography underpins the quantification of ventricular function, yet manual selection of these key frames is subjective and introduces clinically significant inter-operator variability. Recent self-supervised methods either prescribe strict periodic trajectories or learn an unconstrained low-dimensional motion subspace from reconstruction or registration objectives. The former offers interpretability but imposes restrictive assumptions on temporal progression, whereas the latter leaves cardiac phase implicit and ED/ES must be recovered through post-hoc geometric processing of the learned trajectory. We translate the physiological observation that cardiac phase is a one-dimensional signal into a prior by constraining the latent motion component to a single-parameter latent orbit, i.e., a global linear trajectory in latent space indexed by a bounded scalar phase variable. Mapping this variable through a sinusoidal nonlinearity yields an oscillatory motion signal with consistent temporal ordering, enabling direct identification of ED and ES from the learned phase signal. This inductive bias allows the model to capture an interpretable representation of the cardiac cycle, while maintaining flexibility to capture irregular heartbeats. Trained on EchoNet-Dynamic without annotations, our minimal single-parameter cardiac phase model learns an effective latent orbit, significantly improves upon the previous state of the art in ED localisation and matches it in ES localisation while using a more constrained representation and fewer training epochs. This demonstrates that a principled physiological inductive bias can match or exceed the performance of more complex representations. Code is available at: https://github.com/BonniciJ/OrbitalEcho/

</details>


### [66] [Multimodal Taxonomic Conditioning for Generative Plankton Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11673)
*Daniela Ivanova,Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对自动浮游生物成像数据集的极端长尾问题，提出以分类学（taxonomy）为条件生成合成浮游生物图像：先用排序对比目标在大型浮游生物语料上适配 CLIP 编码器（支持深层、不规则的层级分类体系），冻结后用于条件化一个参数高效的扩散 Transformer，并从分布保真度与下游分类器实用性两方面评估合成样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动浮游生物成像流程会产生严重长尾的数据集：少数稀有类群（往往是最具生态学意义、需要监测的类群）图像数量极少，无法支撑分类器的可靠训练与评估。生态监测与生物多样性研究因此受限于数据稀缺。作者希望用可控的合成数据来补充稀有类群样本，从而缓解长尾分布带来的训练与评测困难。

Method: 1) 条件化方案基于文本/类别语义：将 CLIP 编码器在一个大型浮游生物语料上适配，采用排序对比目标（ranked contrastive objective），并将其扩展到深层、不平整（ragged）的分类学层级结构，从而在条件中编码细粒度、多层级的类别语义。2) 该编码器随后被冻结，用于条件化一个参数高效（parameter-efficient）的扩散 Transformer，以生成与分类学标签一致的合成浮游生物图像。3) 评估分两条线：合成样本的分布保真度，以及作为训练/评测补充时对下游分类器的效用。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数据集名称、样本规模、评价指标数值或基线对比结果。评估设计明确包含两个方面：(a) 分布层面的保真度度量，用于衡量合成图像与真实浮游生物分布的对齐程度；(b) 下游分类器效用，即用合成样本（尤其是稀有类群）补充训练/评测后分类性能的变化。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于把分层分类学知识引入生成条件：通过适配 CLIP 的排序对比学习适配深层不规则的分类体系，并以参数高效扩散 Transformer 实现条件生成，为长尾浮游生物数据集提供稀有类群样本。其意义在于用生成式数据增强支撑生态相关的稀有类群识别。局限方面，摘要未报告定量结果、真实/合成分布差距的具体表现、以及是否存在生成伪影或分类学条件泄漏等问题，需阅读正文核实。

Abstract: Automated plankton imaging produces severely long-tailed datasets, where the rare taxa of greatest ecological interest have too few images to train or evaluate classifiers reliably. We generate synthetic plankton imagery conditioned on taxonomy: a CLIP encoder is adapted on a large plankton corpus with a ranked contrastive objective extended to deep, ragged taxonomies, then frozen to condition a parameter-efficient diffusion transformer. We evaluate synthetic sample quality on distributional fidelity and downstream classifier utility.

</details>


### [67] [Single-Stream Multi-Feature Fusion with Temporal Robustness for Gait Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11680)
*Shirong Lyu,Silu Quan,Yixuan Ding,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SV-GCN：一种单流多特征融合、具备时间不变性的3D骨架步态情感识别框架。通过引入帧内相对运动特征与全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块，实现帧率无关性与跨序列长度/帧率的强泛化能力，并在E-Gait数据集上达到与SOTA相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于3D骨架的步态情感识别面临三大难题：(1) 数据标注成本高、样本稀缺；(2) 在异构数据（不同采集设备、帧率、序列长度）上泛化能力差；(3) 现有方法对帧率敏感、依赖多流架构，复杂度高，难以在大型骨架动作识别数据集上做预训练迁移。解决帧率敏感性和异构泛化问题，是该领域走向大规模预训练与实用化的关键前提。

Method: 提出SV-GCN单流多特征融合框架，核心创新有三点：(1) 引入帧内相对运动特征（intra-frame relative motion），消除对帧率的敏感性，替代易受采样率影响的绝对运动表示；(2) 在浅层嵌入异构线索（heterogeneous cues），实现早期融合，避免多流架构的冗余与计算开销；(3) 针对变长序列设计全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块（global mask-guided valid-frame spatio-temporal graph convolution），据作者称是该领域首次引入帧率不敏感性机制。

Result: 在E-Gait数据集上实验，性能与当前SOTA方法相当；同时在不同序列长度和帧率条件下展现出强泛化能力，验证了所提相对运动特征与掩码引导图卷积对帧率变化与序列长度变化的鲁棒性。论文还指出该方法为在大型骨架动作识别数据集上进行预训练提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于以单流架构同时解决多特征融合、帧率不变性与变长序列建模三个问题，降低了多流方法的复杂度，并首次在步态情感识别领域引入帧率不敏感机制。意义在于提升了异构数据泛化能力，为跨数据集预训练奠定基础。局限在于主要验证集中于E-Gait单一数据集，缺少在更多异构数据集上的大规模预训练与跨域迁移实验，帧率不敏感性的理论保证与边界条件仍有待进一步论证。

Abstract: 3D skeleton-based gait emotion recognition faces high annotation costs, data scarcity, and poor generalization on heterogeneous data. This paper proposes SV-GCN, a single-stream multi-feature fusion framework with temporal invariance. We introduce intra-frame relative motion features to eliminate frame-rate sensitivity and embed heterogeneous cues at shallow layers, enabling early fusion without multi-stream complexity. For variable-length sequences, we design a global mask-guided valid-frame spatio-temporal graph convolution module, introducing frame-rate insensitivity for the first time in this domain. On the E-Gait dataset, our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art while demonstrating strong generalization across varying sequence lengths and frame rates, offering a viable pathway for pre-training on large-scale skeleton-based action recognition datasets.

</details>


### [68] [Spectral Adapters for Segment Anything Model-based Segmentation of Colorectal Liver Metastases in Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11703)
*Ramtin Mojtahedi,Mohammad Hamghalam,Jacob J. Peoples,Natalie Gangai,Mithat Gonen,Yun Shin Chun,HyunSeon Christine Kang,Richard K. G. Do,Amber L. Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文为Segment Anything Model（SAM）提出两种参数高效的光谱适配器——DiSECT（Directional Spectral Adapter）与SiGA（Spectral Instance-Guided Adapter），用于对比增强CT中结直肠癌肝转移（CRLM）的病灶分割，在极少可训练参数下达到接近3D nnU-Net的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRLM在对比增强CT中的精确分割对疗效评估、手术规划和随访至关重要，但医学分割标注成本高、数据规模有限，直接微调SAM等大模型代价大且易过拟合。因此需要一种参数高效、能保留SAM预训练能力、同时适配医学影像光谱/结构特性的适配方案。论文的动机在于验证光谱约束与实例级门控能否在极低可训练参数预算下将SAM有效迁移至CRLM分割任务。

Method: 论文冻结SAM主干权重，提出两种残差式光谱适配器。(1) DiSECT：对冻结权重做奇异值分解（SVD），将残差更新约束在主奇异值对应的主导光谱方向上，从而使适配集中在信息量最大的子空间，显著减少可训练参数量。(2) SiGA：在残余更新基础上引入全局门控与输入条件门控，通过多层感知机（MLP）生成门控系数，实现实例自适应的光谱调制。两者均属于低秩/光谱参数高效微调（PEFT）范式，与LoRA、QLoRA、卷积适配器（CAD）形成对比。实验覆盖单点、三点、边界框和无提示（no-prompt）四种提示范式。

Result: 在446例对比增强CT体数据（355训练/91测试）上评估，指标为Dice、IoU和HD95。SiGA在单点提示下取得最佳性能：Dice 0.77、IoU 0.69、HD95 35.39 mm；在无提示推理下达到Dice 0.76、IoU 0.68、HD95 46.76 mm，与3D nnU-Net基线（Dice 0.758）相当。DiSECT仅使用0.14M可训练参数，表明光谱适配可在极小参数预算下保持强分割精度。对比对象包括LoRA、QLoRA、CAD及nnU-Net。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出DiSECT与SiGA两种光谱适配器，证明基于SVD主导方向约束与实例门控的PEFT策略能高效地将SAM迁移至CRLM分割，在极少可训练参数下达到与专用3D nnU-Net相当或更优的精度，并支持无提示推理。意义在于为医学影像基础模型适配提供了参数高效的新思路。潜在局限：评测限于单一任务（CRLM）与单一模态（对比增强CT），数据规模较小（446例），HD95在无提示下相对较高，光谱约束与门控的通用性尚需在多器官、多模态及更多边界模糊小病灶场景中进一步验证。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of colorectal liver metastases (CRLM) in contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) is important for response assessment, surgical planning, and follow-up. We propose two parameter-efficient spectral adapters for the Segment Anything Model (SAM): the Directional Spectral Adapter (DiSECT) and Spectral Instance-Guided Adapter (SiGA). DiSECT uses singular value decomposition of frozen weights to constrain residual updates to leading spectral directions, while SiGA adds global and input-conditioned gating through a multilayer perceptron. We evaluate these methods on 446 contrast-enhanced CT volumes (355 training, 91 testing) and compare them with LoRA, QLoRA, convolutional adapters (CAD), and a 3D nnU-Net baseline. Experiments consider single-point, three-point, bounding-box, and no-prompt regimes. SiGA achieves the best single-point performance with a Dice score of 0.77, IoU of 0.69, and HD95 of 35.39 mm. Under no-prompt inference, SiGA reaches 0.76 Dice, 0.68 IoU, and 46.76 mm HD95, comparable to the nnU-Net baseline (0.758 Dice). DiSECT uses only 0.14 million trainable parameters. These results show that spectral adapters can efficiently adapt SAM for CRLM segmentation while retaining strong accuracy with limited trainable parameters.

</details>


### [69] [Language-Augmented Semantic Priors for B-Spline Surface Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11708)
*Yunzhong Lou,Yusheng Luo,Jiahao Li,Yu Song,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LASP 提出一个语言增强的语义先验框架，利用大语言模型从 CAD 过程化建模历史中推理出结构化、求解器可用的 B 样条先验参数，作为语义推理层叠加在现有几何内核之上，引导传统 B 样条拟合走向更符合设计意图的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B 样条与 NURBS 曲面是现代 CAD 系统的数学基础，但传统几何内核在曲面拟合与参数化时仍严重依赖预先设定的启发式初始化。同时，建模历史中编码的过程化语义与设计意图在几何生成阶段基本被忽略。这种脱节导致高层设计意图与求解器可执行的几何配置之间存在鸿沟，拟合结果往往次优且语义不一致。论文的动机正是弥补这一鸿沟，将语言层面的设计意图转化为可作为归纳偏置的几何先验。

Method: LASP 不修改几何内核本身，而是作为一个位于现有求解器之上的语义推理层。流程上：首先将建模历史翻译为丰富的文本描述，捕捉设计意图、几何上下文与功能关系；随后用微调后的 LLM 预测结构化的 B 样条先验参数。训练采用两阶段方案，融合局部几何规律与长程上下文依赖，使先验既具可解释性又保持语义连贯，从而为传统 B 样条拟合过程提供归纳信号。

Result: 论文通过实验证明，相较于传统机器学习方案，语言驱动的推理能够作为几何求解的强大归纳偏置，使拟合结果更准确地体现预期设计目标并具有更高的语义连贯性，从而确立了语言引导几何优化的新范式。摘要未给出具体数据集、评估指标与定量数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 LASP 框架，把 LLM 引入 CAD 曲面拟合，用语言推理生成结构化 B 样条先验，建立语言引导几何优化的新范式，且以非侵入方式兼容现有求解器。局限方面：摘要未披露具体数据集、评价指标与量化结果，先验质量对建模历史文本化的完整性与 LLM 微调效果存在依赖，对复杂工业级几何的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The use of B-splines and Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines surfaces constitutes the mathematical foundation of contemporary computer-aided design (CAD) systems. Despite long-term progress, geometric kernels in traditional CAD still rely heavily on predetermined heuristic initialization for surface fitting and parameterization. Meanwhile, the procedural semantics and design intent encoded in modeling histories are largely ignored during geometry generation. This disconnect creates a gap between high-level design intent and solver-executable geometric configuration, often leading to suboptimal and semantically inconsistent fitting results. To bridge this gap, we introduce LASP, a Language-Augmented Semantic Priors framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to infer structured, solver-usable B-spline priors from procedural modeling histories. Rather than modifying the geometric kernel itself, LASP operates as a semantic reasoning layer above existing solvers. It first translates modeling histories into rich textual descriptions that capture design intent, geometric context, and functional relationships, and then uses a fine-tuned LLM to predict structured B-spline prior parameters. LASP is trained through a two-stage scheme that combines local geometric regularities with long-range contextual dependencies, producing priors that are both interpretable and semantically coherent. This approach furnishes inductive signals that direct the conventional B-spline fitting process toward solutions that more accurately encapsulate the intended design objectives and demonstrate heightened semantic coherence. Compared to traditional machine learning schemes, the experiments demonstrate that language-driven reasoning can serve as a powerful inductive bias for geometric solving, establishing a new paradigm of language-guided geometric optimization in modern CAD systems.

</details>


### [70] [Revisiting Avatar-As-Image: High-Fidelity Registration is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11722)
*Margaret Kostyrko,Yuxuan Xue,Garvita Tiwari,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AvaImg 提出一个多阶段优化管线，为任意穿着扫描生成高保真的 SMPL(-X)+D 配准与 UV 纹理/位移图，并验证该 "Avatar-as-Image" 表示可直接被冻结的 FLUX VAE 编码，从而与图像基础模型兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 3D 穿衣人体表示为人体模型之上的标准化 2D UV 纹理图与位移图，是一种紧凑表示，能让预训练图像网络直接处理、生成和编辑 3D 化身。但这一表示只有在扫描被高保真配准并建立对应关系时才有用。作者认为，此前 UV 类方法质量受限的根本原因正是这一前提从未被满足：目前没有公开方法能从任意穿衣扫描中产生带 UV 纹理的高保真 SMPL(-X)+D 配准。该配准缺口既限制了穿衣人体重建精度，也阻碍了 2D 生成先验向 3D 化身生成的迁移。

Method: AvaImg 是一个多阶段优化管线，核心包括三部分。其一，使用带符号绕数（signed winding numbers）强制施加"身体位于衣物内部"（body-inside-clothing）约束，保证配准的物理合理性；为使其可行，设计了三级效率级联，将运行时间降低约 10 倍、存储节省约 95%。其二，采用由粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的位移优化，恢复精细表面细节。其三，将结果表达为 UV 纹理图与位移图形式的标准 "Avatar-as-Image" 表示，并使用冻结的 FLUX VAE 自动编码这些 UV 图，以验证其与图像基础模型的兼容性及是否落在自然图像分布内。

Result: 在六个数据集上进行评估，AvaImg 在身体拟合、形状估计与表面配准三方面均优于所有基线，生成的带纹理配准结果与原始扫描几乎无法区分（PSNR=34.48dB）。通过冻结的 FLUX VAE 编码 UV 图，仅带来 0.76mm 的额外 Chamfer 误差（相对扫描），并显示所得特征图位于自然图像分布之内，支持将 2D 生成先验用于 3D 化身生成。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：首次提供从任意穿衣扫描产生高保真 SMPL(-X)+D 配准与 UV 纹理的公开方法，通过绕数约束与效率级联解决了配准核心难题，并实证 "Avatar-as-Image" 表示与图像基础模型（FLUX VAE）兼容，为基于 2D 生成先验的 3D 化身生成铺路。潜在局限包括：仍依赖多阶段优化管线，计算流程较复杂；论文未涉及事件相机、时序建模或目标检测跟踪等方向，主要面向静态 3D 人体扫描配准与化身表示。

Abstract: The representation of 3D clothed humans as standardized 2D UV texture and displacement maps over an underlying body model has long been studied. This compact representation is enticing as it enables pretrained image networks to process, generate, and edit 3D avatars, but is only useful if scans are accurately aligned and brought into correspondence via high-fidelity registration. This prerequisite has never been met, which we argue explains the limited quality of prior UV-based methods for clothed humans. Despite its significance, no public method produces high-fidelity SMPL(-X)+D registrations with UV texture from arbitrary clothed scans. We present AvaImg, a multi-stage optimization pipeline, to close this gap: it enforces body-inside-clothing constraint via signed winding numbers, made viable by a three-level efficiency cascade (~10x runtime reduced, ~95% storage saved), and recovers fine surface detail using coarse-to-fine displacement optimization. AvaImg outperforms all baselines in body fitting, shape estimation, and surface registration across six datasets, yielding textured registrations near-indistinguishable from scans (PSNR=34.48dB). For validation of AvaImg's Avatar-as-Image representation as imminently compatible with image foundation models, we auto-encode our UV maps via the frozen FLUX VAE. This achieves only 0.76mm added Chamfer error relative to scan and shows that the resulting maps lie within natural-image distributions, supporting the use of 2D generative priors for 3D avatar generation. Code, data, and Singularity containers will be at https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg.

</details>


### [71] [Logit Refiner: Improving Visual Autoregressive Models via Intra-Scale Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11804)
*Meimingwei Li,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出视觉自回归模型(VAR)的"下一尺度预测"并行解码本质上是一种均值场(mean-field)式近似，丢弃了同尺度token之间的空间依赖，导致局部不连贯；为此提出轻量级 Logit Refiner，以顺序自回归方式在同一尺度内恢复依赖关系，仅增加约10%参数、不到5%训练算力，即可即插即用地提升任意预训练 VAR 的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR 通过"下一尺度预测"生成图像，在每个尺度内并行生成所有 token，这种解码方式带来高效并行性，但论文指出：同一尺度内 token 被独立采样，等价于一个均值场式近似，从而丢弃了同尺度 token 间的空间依赖。这导致即使增大骨干网络容量，局部仍会出现不连贯的样本。作者强调这是"解码规则本身"的局限，而非模型容量或训练不足，因此需要一种针对解码过程的修正方法，而非简单扩大模型。

Method: 核心方法是 Logit Refiner：一个轻量级自回归模块，通过在同一尺度内以顺序方式、基于冻结(frozen)骨干特征的条件来依次采样 token，从而恢复同尺度内部依赖。其设计要点包括：(1) 骨架网络保持冻结，Refiner 仅作为附加解码模块，因此可插入任意预训练 VAR checkpoint，无需重训练；(2) 参数开销仅约10%，训练算力不到基座模型的5%；(3) 通过受控消融实验，将增益归因于"联合的尺度内采样"(joint intra-scale sampling)，而非额外容量或额外训练。这使得 Refiner 在保持 VAR 高效并行结构的同时，补足其解码规则的理论缺陷。

Result: 在类别条件 ImageNet 256x256 上，从 310M 到 2B 参数的多种骨干网络进行评测，Refiner 一致地提升生成质量；其中 1.1B 参数模型借助 Refiner 的生成质量可超越两倍规模(约2B)的模型。消融实验确认尺度内联合采样是关键因素。方法还可推广到文本到图像(text-to-image)生成，表明均值场瓶颈在多种 VAR 变体中普遍存在，且所提方法可有效缓解。评测指标为生成质量(ImageNet 生成通常采用 FID 等)，论文强调了跨骨干的一致性改进。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：从理论上将 VAR 的尺度内并行解码识别为均值场近似并揭示其空间依赖缺失的瓶颈；提出轻量、即插即用、无需重训练的 Logit Refiner 来恢复尺度内依赖；并在多规模骨干与文本到图像任务上验证其一致有效性。其意义在于说明生成质量的提升可来自修正解码规则而非单纯扩大模型。潜在局限包括：引入顺序采样会削弱 VAR 原有的并行效率(推理开销增加)；主要验证设定为类别条件 ImageNet 与文本到图像，其在中大规模分辨率、视频或其他模态上的泛化性尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive Models (VAR) generate images through next-scale prediction, producing all tokens within each scale in parallel. We show that this parallel decoding constitutes a mean-field-style approximation that discards spatial dependencies among same-scale tokens, causing locally incoherent samples regardless of backbone capacity -- a limitation of the decoding rule. Addressing this limitation, we introduce the Logit Refiner, a lightweight autoregressive module that restores intra-scale dependencies by sequentially sampling tokens conditioned on frozen backbone features. Adding only ~10% parameters and less than 5% of the base model's training compute, it plugs into any pretrained VAR checkpoint without retraining. Controlled ablations isolate joint intra-scale sampling -- rather than additional capacity or training -- as the critical ingredient. Across backbones from 310M to 2B parameters on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256, the refiner consistently improves generation quality, enabling a 1.1B-parameter model to surpass one twice its size. The approach further generalizes to text-to-image generation, confirming that the mean-field bottleneck persists across VAR variants and is effectively alleviated by our method.
  Project page: https://compvis.github.io/logit-refiner/

</details>


### [72] [Guided Super-Resolution of Digital Elevation Models with Diffusion-Based Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11886)
*Armand Mihai Nicolicioiu,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Daniel Panangian,Ksenia Bittner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于去噪扩散模型（denoising diffusion）的引导式超分辨率方法，利用高分辨率多光谱影像作为引导，将商用卫星提供的5米分辨率粗粒度数字表面模型（DSM）提升至0.5米分辨率，从而在无需昂贵数据采集的前提下重建高精度城市表面几何与结构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率DSM在都市分析、三维建筑重建与基础设施监测中至关重要，但其获取成本高、流程复杂，导致数据稀缺。相比之下，商业卫星提供的粗分辨率DSM广泛可得，高分辨率光学影像也日益丰富。由此产生了空间分辨率不匹配的问题：即拥有丰富的光谱细节影像，却只有粗糙的高程数据。论文旨在利用影像中可见的细节（如清晰轮廓、屋顶结构）来弥补高程图的不足，实现低成本的高分辨率表面重建。

Method: 方法核心为引导式超分辨率（guided super-resolution），采用去噪扩散概率模型作为生成框架。与传统的插值或滤波方法不同，扩散模型能够将仅在影像中可见的结构信息（如锐利边缘、精细屋顶结构）迁移（transfer）到高程图中。高分辨率光谱影像作为引导条件（guidance），为扩散过程提供空间结构先验，从而在超分辨率过程中恢复0.5米分辨率的表面细节。论文强调了使用"基础影像先验"（foundational image priors）来重建高分辨率表面模型的能力。

Result: 实验在中欧多个城市的数据上进行，评估指标关注生成DSM的结构细节质量与表面几何精度。结果表明，与传统的插值或滤波技术相比，所提方法能够重建更准确的结构细节，生成高质量的DSM，改善了建筑物的轮廓与屋顶几何。实验验证了引导式超分辨率结合基础影像先验在重建高分辨率表面模型方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出了一种基于扩散模型的引导式DSM超分辨率框架，利用广泛可得的粗粒度DSM与高分辨率影像重建高质量0.5米表面模型，缓解了数据获取瓶颈问题。其意义在于展示了基础影像先验在遥感表面重建中的应用价值。潜在局限包括：对引导影像质量的依赖、跨城市/跨区域泛化能力、定量评估细节未在摘要中充分给出，以及在稀疏或缺乏影像引导区域的性能表现尚不明确。

Abstract: High-resolution digital surface models (DSMs) play an important role in urban analysis, 3D building reconstruction, and infrastructure monitoring, yet their availability remains limited due to the high cost and complexity of data acquisition. In contrast, coarse DSMs from commercial satellite missions are widely accessible, and high-resolution optical imagery is increasingly available from aerial and satellite platforms. We address the resulting mismatch in spatial resolution and propose a DSM superresolution approach that enhances 5 m DSMs to 0.5 m resolution, using guidance from high-resolution spectral images. Our method employs denoising diffusion to transfer information that is visible only in the image, like crisp outlines and detailed roof structures, into the elevation maps. In this way, surface details are reconstructed more accurately than with conventional interpolation or filtering techniques. Experiments on several cities in Central Europe demonstrate that the proposed approach produces high-quality DSMs with improved structural detail and accurate surface geometry. Our results highlight the potential of guided super-resolution with foundational image priors as a means of reconstructing high-resolution surface models.

</details>


### [73] [3D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11894)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 3DPS（3D Point Splatting），首个面向毫米波雷达的可微点渲染器，直接从雷达方程立体角形式推导。每个有向 3D 点携带 ITU-R P.2040 材料模型，以闭式求解，复数相量经预计算点扩散函数（PSF）splat 进距离门。推理输出为复数，使其与具体数据格式无关，同一优化场景无需重训练即可经 FFT 管线生成 ADC、CRP 和 RA 多种输出。在六个 ColoRadar 室外场景上，RA 图像平均 Pearson 相关性达 0.587，为三个光学 NVS 基线（RadarSplat、Radar Fields、DART）的 1.7–5.2 倍，单张 RTX 4090 上每场景训练约 3 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达新视角合成（NVS）需要同时满足三个性质的渲染器：物理保真、复数输出、多视角可优化。此前方法无法兼得：可微蒙特卡洛（MC）光线追踪直接实现雷达前向模型，具备显式材料建模与复数输出，但无法扩展到 NVS 所需的多视角优化规模；而 NeRF、hash grid、3D Gaussian 等光学 NVS 迁移方案训练快，却丢弃相位信息、以不透明的学习特征替代显式材料建模，因而只能预测功率域的距离-方位（RA）幅度。论文的动机即在于弥合物理真实性与多视角可扩展性之间的这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出 3D Point Splatting（3DPS），由雷达方程的立体角形式直接推导出的可微点渲染器。每个有向（oriented）3D 点显式携带 ITU-R P.2040 材料模型，以闭式方式求值，避免学习不透明特征。产生的复数相量通过预计算的点扩散函数（PSF）splat 到距离门中。核心创新包括：(1) 从物理方程出发的点基元渲染，兼顾显式材料建模与可扩展性；(2) 复数域、PSF 加权的 splatting，使输出与数据格式/product 无关；(3) 同一优化场景可经标准 FFT 管线导出 ADC、复数距离像（CRP）与 RA 三种表示，无需为每种格式重训练。

Result: 在六个 ColoRadar 室外场景上进行评估，指标为留出 RA 图像上的平均 Pearson 相关性。3DPS 达到 0.587，是三个光学 NVS 基线（RadarSplat、Radar Fields、DART）的 1.7–5.2 倍。训练效率高：单张 RTX 4090 上每场景约 3 分钟。由于渲染输出为复数，模型可无需重训练地生成 ADC、CRP 与 RA 多种雷达表示。

Conclusion: 3DPS 是首个面向毫米波雷达、兼顾物理保真、复数输出与多视角可优化的可微点渲染器，通过直接源于雷达方程的点基元与 ITU-R P.2040 材料闭式建模，在保持物理意义的同时实现了高效多视角优化，并在 ColoRadar 上显著超越光学 NVS 迁移基线。其复数乘积无关特性为多格式雷达感知提供了统一渲染基础。潜在局限包括：评估仅限 ColoRadar 六个室外场景与 RA 相关性指标，材料模型受限于 ITU-R P.2040 假设，尚未验证室内、动态场景或下游感知任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving novel view synthesis (NVS) for millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires a renderer that is physically faithful, complex-valued, and multi-viewpoint-tractable. No prior method achieves these three properties simultaneously. Differentiable Monte Carlo (MC) ray tracers implement the radar forward model directly with explicit material modeling and complex outputs, but do not scale to the multi-view optimization NVS demands. Optical-NVS ports of NeRF, hash grids, and 3D Gaussians train fast but discard phase and replace explicit material modeling with opaque learned features, restricting them to power-only range-azimuth (RA) magnitudes. We propose 3D Point Splatting (3DPS), the first differentiable point renderer for radar, derived directly from the standard solid-angle form of the radar equation. Each oriented 3D point carries an ITU-R P.2040 material model, evaluated in closed form, with the resulting complex phasor splatted into range bins through a precomputed point spread function (PSF). The complex-valued output makes the renderer product-agnostic. The same optimized scene yields analog-to-digital converter (ADC), complex range profile (CRP), and RA outputs through standard fast Fourier transform (FFT) pipelines without retraining for each format. On six outdoor ColoRadar scenes, 3DPS reaches 0.587 mean Pearson correlation on held-out RA images. This is between 1.7x and 5.2x the three optical-NVS baselines (RadarSplat, Radar Fields, DART). Training takes approximately 3 minutes per scene on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [74] [Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand: Visual-Need Routing for Budget-Aware Agentic Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11899)
*Weitong Cai,Hang Zhang,Yukai Huang,Yiqiao Xie,Shan Gao,Jiankang Deng,Songcen Xu,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand (CFD)，一个预算感知的边缘-云智能体（agentic）框架，用于长视频理解。其核心洞察是"视觉-文本对偶性"：语言记忆更擅长承载长程时序结构，而像素对细粒度属性感知不可替代。CFD 在边缘端仅执行一次离线字幕生成，构建"事件级故事骨架 + 片段级微日志"的双轨叙事索引并缓存复用；在查询时由云端 MLLM 以"故事优先"循环推理，并通过轻量级 Visual-Need Router 实现按需、有界的帧检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上理解长达数小时的长视频，需要在严格的计算与带宽预算下进行推理。现有方法存在两难：对视觉 token 做下采样虽然降低了成本，但破坏了时序结构；而纯文本的视频记忆虽然高效，却丢失了细粒度的视觉属性（如外观、画面文字、属性消歧）。因此，关键问题是如何在有限预算下同时保留长程时序结构与属性级视觉感知能力，并避免对视频进行重复的昂贵视觉处理。

Method: CFD 是一个边缘-云协同的智能体框架，包含三部分：(1) 边缘端单次离线字幕生成（caption-once），构建双轨叙事索引——事件级故事骨架（event-level story skeleton）与片段级微日志（clip-level micro-log），缓存后跨查询复用，无需重新 caption；(2) 云端 MLLM 在查询时以"story-first"循环对索引进行推理；(3) 核心创新为轻量级 Visual-Need Router，这是一个按查询条件的门控模块（per-query gating），仅对感知类问题（外观、屏幕文字、属性消歧）触发有界的关键帧检索，而时序-结构类问题则保持在语言空间中处理。该路由器将"视觉访问"变为一等公民的、查询条件的成本项，从而无论视频多长，都能对每次查询的帧消耗设定上限。

Result: 论文在长视频基准（long-video benchmarks）上进行实验，结果表明 CFD 在准确率与效率之间取得了良好的权衡（strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs），同时显著降低了在线视觉处理开销。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标数值或消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了"视觉-文本对偶性"这一设计洞察，并将其落地为 CFD 框架：通过 caption-once 的缓存复用避免重复字幕、通过 story-first 推理保持长程时序结构、通过 Visual-Need Router 将视觉检索限制在真正需要像素的查询上，从而在边缘-云设定下大幅削减在线视觉计算并设定了每查询帧预算上限。可能局限包括：依赖离线字幕质量，细粒度视觉属性受限于 caption 表达能力；关键帧检索的有效性依赖路由器门控的准确性；摘要未说明对极端长视频或开放式问答的可扩展性与具体性能边界。

Abstract: Long-video understanding on edge devices must reason over hours of content under tight compute and bandwidth budgets. Subsampling visual tokens loses temporal structure, while text-only video memories lose fine-grained visual attributes. We observe a visual-textual duality: language memories carry long-range temporal structure better than dense frames, while pixels remain decisive for attribute-level perception. Building on this insight, we propose Caption-once, Frames-onDemand (CFD), a budget-aware edge-cloud agentic framework. The edge runs a single offline captioning pass that builds a dual-track narrative index, an event-level story skeleton plus a clip-level micro-log, cached and reused across queries without re-captioning. At query time, a cloud-side MLLM reasons over the index in a story-first loop centered on a lightweight Visual-Need Router: a per-query gating module that triggers bounded keyframe retrieval only for perceptual questions (appearance, on-screen text, attribute disambiguation) and keeps temporal-structural questions in language space. The router turns visual access into a first-class, query-conditioned cost, capping per-query frame consumption regardless of video length. Experiments on long-video benchmarks demonstrate strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs while substantially reducing online visual processing.

</details>


### [75] [SenseNova-U1.5: Towards Native Unified Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11929)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Chenjing Ding,Hanming Deng,Jiangnan Chen,Ruixi Zhang,Ruohui Wang,Wenwen Tong,Xiangyu Fan,Yubo Wang,Yue Zhu,Yuwei Niu,Zhengqi Bai,Zhiqian Lin,Zhitao Yang,Zhongang Cai,Bo Yang,Chen Feng,Chengguang Lv,Guangjia Liu,Guanlin Wang,Hanyu Zhang,Haojia Yu,Hongcan Xiao,Hongli Wang,Huan Wu,Huaping Zhong,Jian Fang,Jianan Fan,Jiaqi Li,Jiefan Lu,Jing Zuo,Jingcheng Ni,Junxiang Xu,Linjun Dai,Mutian Xu,Peishen Yan,Penghao Wu,Ruijie Mao,Ruisi Wang,Shihao Bai,Shuang Yang,Shuya Yang,Shuyan Zheng,Silei Wu,Siying Li,Tao Chu,Tianbo Zhong,Tongxi Zhou,Weichao Luo,Weichen Fan,Wenhao Jia,Wenjie Gao,Xiangli Kong,Yan Li,Yang Yong,Zimo Wen,Zixuan Qian,Wenxiu Sun,Ruihao Gong,Quan Wang,Lewei Lu,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseNova-U1.5 是一个 8B 参数量的原生统一多模态模型（native unified multimodal model），采用 8B-MoT（Mixture-of-Transformers）架构，在无编码器（encoder-free）、无 VAE（VAE-free）的框架内同时完成视觉理解、推理与生成。其核心贡献是通过空间一致的 patch 重建强化视觉接口、以高质量生成/编辑数据与最高 4K 原生分辨率扩展训练，并通过多专家 on-policy 蒸馏整合美学、双语文本渲染、信息图生成与图像编辑等专项能力。论文将开源于训练代码（SFT、RL、on-policy distillation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态系统的主流范式是将视觉理解与视觉生成割裂为独立模块：理解侧依赖预训练视觉编码器（如 CLIP/SigLIP），生成侧依赖 VAE 或扩散解码器。这种"拼接式"设计带来接口不匹配、能力难以互相迁移、难以在统一框架内做端到端推理与创造等问题。论文的动机是验证"原生统一建模"（native unified modelling）的可行性——让同一个端到端模型既能感知、推理，又能规划并生成视觉内容，并期望理解能力能够迁移到视觉规划与创作任务上。同时，视觉保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑等生成质量瓶颈，以及结构化视觉指令的泛化能力，也是该工作重点解决的问题。

Method: 模型层面：SenseNova-U1.5 为 8B 规模 MoT（Mixture-of-Transformers）架构，理解与生成共享统一主干但采用专家化路径，整体为 encoder-free + VAE-free 设计，即不依赖独立视觉编码器与 VAE 潜在空间。视觉接口层面：采用"空间一致的 patch 重建"（spatially coherent patch reconstruction）来强化视觉 token 与像素空间的对齐质量。训练层面：扩增训练规模并引入精心筛选的生成与编辑数据，改进任务表述（task formulation），引入结构化提示增强（structural prompt enhancement），并支持最高 4K 原生分辨率。后训练层面：为视觉美学、双语文本渲染、信息图生成、图像编辑分别优化专项专家，再通过多专家 on-policy 蒸馏（multi-expert on-policy distillation）将各专家能力整合回单一统一模型，以缓解多能力间的冲突。

Result: 论文报告了广泛评测，覆盖图像保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑、交错生成（interleaved generation）以及指令跟随等维度。主要发现包括：SenseNova-U1.5 在上述多方面取得显著提升，同时在编辑任务中能保持主体身份（subject identity）、几何结构与未修改区域的一致性；尽管其生成数据中结构化格式的曝光有限，模型仍能有效泛化到长、复杂、结构化的视觉指令，说明多模态理解能力可迁移至视觉规划与创作。论文未在摘要中给出具体数据集名称、指标数值与对比基线，需以正文结果为准。此外工作承诺开源 SFT、强化学习与 on-policy 蒸馏的训练代码。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献在于：提出并验证了 8B-MoT 的 encoder-free / VAE-free 原生统一多模态架构，通过空间一致 patch 重建与多专家 on-policy 蒸馏，在统一框架内兼顾视觉理解、推理与高质量生成/编辑；并证明了理解能力向视觉创作迁移的潜力，将原生统一建模定位为通向"感知—推理—创造"一体化系统的可行路径。可能局限：摘要未披露与 SOTA 生成模型/统一模型的具体量化对比、训练数据规模与算力开销、以及 4K 原生分辨率下的推理效率；此外，encoder-free/VAE-free 设计在细粒度重建与高保真生成上的上限、以及结构化指令泛化的稳定性，仍需更系统的验证。

Abstract: We launch SenseNova-U1.5, an 8B-MoT native unified multimodal model that understands, reasons about, and generates visual content within an encoder-free and VAE-free architecture. We strengthen its visual interface through spatially coherent patch reconstruction and scale its training with carefully curated generation and editing data, improved task formulation, structural prompt enhancement, and native resolutions of up to 4K. For post-training, we optimize specialized experts for visual aesthetics, bilingual text rendering, infographic generation, and image editing, and consolidate their capabilities through multi-expert on-policy distillation. Across extensive evaluations, SenseNova-U1.5 largely advances image fidelity, text rendering, complex composition, multi-reference editing, and interleaved generation, while improving instruction following and preserving subject identity, geometry, and unmodified regions. Despite limited exposure to structured formats in its generation data, SenseNova-U1.5 generalizes effectively to long, complex, and structured visual instructions, further proving that multimodal understanding can transfer to visual planning and creation. Together, these findings position native unified modelling as a promising path towards systems that perceive, reason and create within a fully end-to-end framework. We will open-source training code, including supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation.

</details>

### [76] [Threshold-Based Selection for Continuous Optimization: A Leaf-Abscission Instantiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10588)
*Nasser Khalili*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文将"阈值式选择"形式化为一种评估门控（evaluation-gating）架构：仅在上下文压力超过个体内在强度时，才生成并评估替代解。基于此提出"叶片脱落优化"（LAO），并通过因子实验将其多层设计精简为 LAO-Core，在 CEC 2017 基准上以相等评估预算取得中等偏上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注的并非视觉或事件相机任务，而是元启发式/群体智能优化的算法设计问题。核心动机是：许多优化算法堆叠了大量辅助机制（多样性调节、环境压力、季节性信号等），但这些组件的独立贡献往往缺乏严格验证。作者希望通过一种生物学类比（叶片脱落：只有当环境压力超过叶柄的内在连接强度时叶子才脱落）来统一解释"阈值式替换"这一机制，并借因子实验剔除冗余设计，从而回答"哪些组件真正有效"这一优化算法工程中的关键问题。

Method: 1）理论形式化：将每个候选解（incumbent）视为在变异前接受阈值测试，仅当"上下文压力 > 内在强度"时才触发替代解的生成与评估，形成"评估门控"架构。
2）LAO 实例化：包含基于排名的强度（rank-based strength）、物候季节性信号（phenological seasonal signal）、多样性调制（diversity modulation）、环境压力（environmental pressure）与基础再生长核（base regrowth kernel）等组件。
3）消融方法：在维度 10、CEC 2017 测试集上采用 2^4 区组因子设计（blocked two-to-the-fourth-power factorial），系统评估各辅助层的独立与交互效应。
4）精度控制：因子分析将原多层设计精简为一个简约核心 LAO-Core。

Result: 实验基于 CEC 2017 基准函数，评估指标为 Friedman 平均排名与最终误差。主要发现：（1）因子实验表明"漂移（drift）"组件有害，其余三个辅助层无稳健的独立增益证据；（2）在维度 10、30、50 且评估预算相等的条件下，LAO-Core 在九种优化器中取得第三好的平均 Friedman 排名；（3）四档预算扫描显示相对性能依赖预算规模，自适应差分进化（adaptive DE）类基线在大预算下相对优势上升；（4）九格"维度-预算"分析既未支持"仅由每维评估预算决定性能"的规律，也未发现统计显著的维度-预算交互作用；（5）配对干预实验显示多样性调制改变了运行后期的替换行为，但在所测预算下对最终误差无显著影响。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将阈值式选择抽象为通用的"评估门控"机制，并以 LAO 为实例，通过严格的因子实验得到简约而有效的 LAO-Core。其意义在于强调优化算法设计中"组件独立贡献需经验证"的方法论，以及竞争力的"情境限定性"（regime-qualified competitiveness），而非宣称普遍优越性。局限性包括：仅在 CEC 2017 合成基准与有限维度/预算范围内验证；未发现维度-预算交互的显著性证据，说明结论的适用范围有限；多样性调制的作用仅体现在行为层面而非误差层面；作者也明确指出该机制不具备跨情境的普适最优性。

Abstract: This paper formalizes threshold-based selection as an evaluation-gating architecture in which each incumbent is tested before variation and a replacement is generated and evaluated only when contextual pressure exceeds intrinsic strength. The mechanism is instantiated as Leaf Abscission Optimization (LAO), using rank-based strength, a phenological seasonal signal, diversity modulation, environmental pressure, and a base regrowth kernel. A blocked two-to-the-fourth-power factorial analysis at dimension 10 on the CEC 2017 suite reduces the original multi-layer design to a parsimonious core: drift is harmful, while the other three auxiliary layers show no robust independent evidence of benefit. The resulting LAO-Core attains the third-best mean Friedman rank among nine optimizers at dimensions 10, 30, and 50 under the equal evaluation budget. A four-budget sweep shows budget-dependent relative performance, with adaptive differential-evolution baselines gaining relative advantage at larger budgets; the nine-cell dimension-budget analysis establishes neither an evaluation-budget-per-dimension-only law nor a statistically significant dimension-budget interaction. A paired intervention shows that diversity modulation changes late-run replacement behaviour without a detectable effect on final error at the tested budget. The evidence supports LAO as a parsimonious evaluation-gating mechanism with regime-qualified competitiveness, rather than as a generally superior optimizer.

</details>


### [77] [Breaking the Central Bias: Spatially Partitioned Experts for Coordinate-Based Neuroevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11518)
*Romain Claret,Arthur Gygax,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Michael Palma Mendes,Pascal Felber*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文诊断了 Evolvable-Substrate HyperNEAT (ES-HyperNEAT) 在 MNIST 上的失效模式：由于输入像素映射到以原点为中心的坐标空间，进化网络会收敛到中心的小簇输入像素，形成"空间集中偏差"。作者借鉴 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 思想，将输入划分为不重叠的空间段，每段由单独进化的专家网络处理，从而缓解偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES-HyperNEAT 是一种生物启发的间接编码方法，从空间坐标推导神经元位置与连接权重。在 MNIST 上它出现严重失效：所有输入像素映射到以原点为中心的坐标空间后，进化网络倾向于只利用图像中心的一小部分像素，此前工作中平均准确率仅约 21%。核心问题是：这种空间集中偏差究竟是优化过程的偶然产物，还是架构本身带来的固有上限？理解这一点对于判断该编码范式的可用性与可迁移性具有重要意义。

Method: 受 MoE 原理启发，作者将输入图像划分为互不重叠的空间段，每段分配给一个独立进化的专家网络（specialist network），使每个专家只需负责局部区域的特征演化。实验中使用了 13 个这样的专家网络。作者同时设计了两个可推广到 MNIST 之外的工具：一是感受野诊断（receptive-field diagnostic），用于检测"输入覆盖塌缩"（silent input-coverage collapse）；二是空间划分补救方案（spatial-partitioning remedy），用于恢复输入覆盖。此外，作者通过等权重平均（不使用验证数据）来验证增益来源。

Result: 使用 13 个专家网络时，平均准确率达到 43%，相对基线提升 106%。关键发现是：该架构增益并不依赖于数据驱动的聚合方式——即使使用等权重平均（完全不用验证数据），也能带来 70% 的相对提升，说明增益来自划分本身而非加权策略。感受野分析揭示了机制：空间划分迫使进化过程在整幅图像上发现特征，活跃像素覆盖率从 4% 提升到 79%。不过，绝对准确率仍低于梯度训练基线。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是：将 ES-HyperNEAT 在 MNIST 上的失效归因于中心偏差（central bias）而非进化搜索本身，并提出基于空间划分的架构性补救方案，同时给出感受野诊断工具。其意义在于提示该偏差具有架构层面的根源，且划分策略可有效恢复输入覆盖。局限性在于：绝对准确率仍低于梯度下降训练的基线，实验主要在 MNIST 这一诊断性基准上开展，向更复杂任务（如事件视觉、检测等）的泛化尚待验证。

Abstract: Evolvable-Substrate HyperNEAT (ES-HyperNEAT), a bio-inspired indirect encoding that determines neuron placement and connection weights from spatial coordinates, exhibits a failure mode on MNIST as a diagnostic benchmark. Because input pixels map to a coordinate space centered at the origin, evolved networks converge on a small central cluster of input pixels, a spatial-concentration bias; prior work observed only 21% mean accuracy in this regime. Is this bias an optimization artifact or an architectural ceiling? Inspired by Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) principles, we partition the input into non-overlapping spatial segments, each assigned to a separately evolved specialist network. With 13 such experts, this design reaches 43% mean accuracy, a 106% relative improvement over the baseline. The architectural gain does not depend on data-driven aggregation: equal-weighted averaging, which uses no validation data, already yields a 70% improvement; the gain comes from partitioning, not the weighting. Receptive-field analysis shows the mechanism: partitioning forces evolution to discover features across the entire image, expanding active pixel coverage from 4% to 79%. Absolute accuracy stays below gradient-trained baselines, but the relative gain points to central bias, not the evolutionary search. Two tools are designed to generalize beyond MNIST: a receptive-field diagnostic for silent input-coverage collapse, and a spatial-partitioning remedy that restores coverage.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将Krotov-Hopfield二分架构（称为类H）统一为可用统计物理方法分析的框架，用每层对应的拉格朗日量来刻画网络。通过副本方法（replica method）揭示：多项式存储负载下retrieval更新等价于softmax注意力的不同极限，恢复容量有闭式解且可见神经元（Ising vs 球面）差异源于可见熵；指数负载下则可映射到随机能量模型（REM）计数，出现顺磁、凝聚与冻结相。核心洞见是类H将retrieval分解为两个正交角色：可见层拉格朗日量决定稳定性，隐藏层拉格朗日量决定存储尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆可视为无序多体系统中的吸引子动力学，而其高阶与指数扩展会把retrieval更新变成softmax注意力。此前多项式与指数两类regime分别用不同方法分析，缺乏一个共同架构来回答"究竟是什么决定了存储尺度"这一根本问题。作者希望建立一个统一的类H框架，把存储容量、相变结构与注意力机制联系起来，从而厘清稳定性与存储尺度分别由什么控制。

Method: 作者研究Krotov-Hopfield的二分（bipartite）架构——类H：每一层由一个拉格朗日量固定，将隐藏神经元视为retrieval的序参量。对多项式负载使用副本方法推导副本对称相图与闭式容量表达式，发现串扰矩（crosstalk moment）对Ising与球面可见神经元是共同的，两者差异仅来自可见熵。对softmax隐藏层（指数负载）引入copy representation，将热力学映射为随机能量模型（REM）的计数问题，得到顺磁、凝聚与冻结三相。进一步分析升温（heating）对retrieval的破坏机制（注意力被量化重分配），以及典型高斯模式在各负载下的亚稳性，并对比两种regime的串扰统计（多项式负载为中心极限、指数负载为大偏差）。

Result: 理论分析（无标准数据集实验，属统计物理/理论工作）：多项式负载下得到副本对称相图与容量的闭式解，表明Ising与球面可见神经元的串扰矩相同、差异仅由可见熵贡献；softmax隐藏层下负载呈指数增长，copy representation给出REM型计数与三相（顺磁/凝聚/冻结）；升温通过注意力的量化重分配破坏retrieval；典型高斯模式在所有负载下均保持亚稳。两regime的串扰统计分别为中心极限与大偏差行为。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出统一的类H框架，用每层拉格朗日量刻画网络并把retrieval的稳定性与存储尺度分离为两条独立轴，同时把高阶/指数Hopfield动力学与softmax注意力、REM计数联系起来，为设计新拉格朗日量提供指导。局限在于工作以统计物理与理论分析为主，未涉及实际数据集或工程部署验证，所依赖的副本对称等假设在部分相区可能失效。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [79] [Solving Few-Shot Multiobjective Multitask Optimization via Iterative Sequential Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11228)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Ananda Phan Iman,Zhao Wei,Jiao Liu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出迭代序贯迁移（Iterative Sequential Transfer, IST），将多任务优化（MTO）重构为一系列序贯迁移优化问题，每次迭代只聚焦单个目标任务，并引入似然信息驱动的任务优先级选择机制，在严格评估预算下提升知识迁移效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化（MTO）通过跨任务知识迁移同时求解多个具有协同关系的优化问题。然而，有效的迁移机制依赖于对齐各任务的精英解分布（elite solution distributions）。在少样本优化（few-shot optimization）场景中，评估预算极为有限，难以充分识别精英解分布，导致迁移失效。这一问题在多目标多任务问题中尤为严重：每个优化器需要逼近连续的 Pareto 流形（Pareto manifold）而非单一最优点，进一步加剧了对解分布对齐的需求与预算限制之间的矛盾。因此，如何在紧迫预算下仍能实现有益迁移，成为 MTO 的关键瓶颈。

Method: 论文提出迭代序贯迁移（IST），核心思路是将 MTO 建模为一连串的序贯迁移优化子问题，而非并行同求解所有任务。每次迭代仅针对一个目标任务集中分配评估预算，从而在有限评估下更充分地刻画该任务的解分布。为决定迭代顺序，作者设计了似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制（likelihood-informed task prioritization），通过估计各任务"已适合进行知识整合"的可能性，优先选择迁移效用最高的任务。该机制旨在最大化每次迁移的收益，避免在尚未充分收敛的任务间进行无效知识传递，从而缓解少样本与多目标设定下的对齐难题。

Result: 论文在基准问题与真实世界问题上进行了实验验证，重点评估在严格评估预算（tight budgets）条件下的优化性能。结果表明，所提 IST 方法在有限评估预算下均能有效工作，验证了序贯迁移与似然驱动任务优先级策略的有效性。（摘要未提供具体数据集名称、评价指标数值及对比方法细节。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 MTO 重新表述为序贯迁移优化序列，提出 IST 框架，并设计似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制以最大化迁移效用，在少样本预算及多目标多任务场景下缓解了精英解分布对齐的瓶颈。其意义在于为评估受限下的多任务知识迁移提供了新范式。潜在局限包括：序贯执行可能牺牲并行效率；方法效果依赖似然估计的准确性；摘要未给出理论收敛性保证与大规模任务的可扩展性分析。

Abstract: Applying knowledge transfer across multiple optimization tasks, multitask optimization (MTO) emerges as a promising approach to solving synergistic optimization tasks simultaneously. However, the development of effective knowledge transfer mechanisms in MTO fundamentally relies on aligning elite solution distributions across tasks. This dependency creates a critical bottleneck in few-shot optimization regimes, as restricted evaluation budgets impede the identification of elite solution distributions required for beneficial transfer. This challenge is exacerbated in multiobjective multitask problems, where each optimizer must approximate a continuous Pareto manifold rather than a single optimal point. This paper introduces Iterative Sequential Transfer (IST) to circumvent this bottleneck. We model MTO as a sequence of sequential transfer optimization problems, concentrating evaluations on a single target per iteration. We propose a likelihood-informed task prioritization mechanism to maximize transfer utility by identifying the task most likely ready for knowledge integration. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world problems verify the effectiveness of the proposed method under tight budgets.

</details>


### [80] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3-Former，一种由大语言模型（LLM）增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架：将船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，通过 LLM 编码并与动态轨迹特征对齐，结合双粒度 MoE 与转向加权损失，实现 1–4 小时长期轨迹预测的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶轨迹预测面临三大挑战：（1）行为多模态性，即相同历史轨迹可能对应多种未来走向；（2）语义信息利用不足，静态属性（船型、尺寸等）与航行意图等先验未被充分挖掘；（3）长期误差累积，随预测时域延长轨迹漂移显著。远洋/内河航运的安全监管、碰撞预警与航路规划对 1–4 小时量级的长期预测有实际需求，而传统方法多依赖纯运动学或历史轨迹外推，难以刻画全局航路规划意图。论文的动机即在于引入语义先验以约束长期预测的不确定性。

Method: 核心思路是"语义引导的层次化预测"。具体包含三部分：（1）统一多模态表示空间：船舶静态语义信息由预训练 LLM 编码，通过自注意力机制与动态轨迹特征对齐，构建语义与运动统一的表示；航行意图亦作为语义先验参与长期建模。（2）双粒度混合专家（MoE）架构：序列级专家建模全局航行趋势（全局航路规划），token 级专家细化局部机动行为（细粒度操纵），二者联合捕捉宏观与微观运动模式。（3）Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy 损失：针对稀疏转向样本的长尾分布进行加权，提升关键机动场景的预测精度。整体构成"高层航行意图 + 局部运动动力学"联合建模的范式。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上进行实验，评估指标为平均位移误差（ADE）与终点位移误差（FDE），预测时域覆盖 1 至 4 小时。结果显示 M3-Former 在各预测时域上一致优于现有 SOTA 基线；在 4 小时预测任务中，相较最强基线 ADE 降低 4.4%、FDE 降低 5.1%。定性分析与消融实验进一步表明：语义融合可有效缓解长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 在复杂航道与航路分叉场景中提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 LLM 增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验对齐、双粒度 MoE 和转向加权损失，建立了语义引导的层次化长期预测范式，在真实 AIS 数据上取得一致且可量化的性能提升。局限方面：摘要仅报告单一丹麦 AIS 数据集的结果，跨区域、跨水域的泛化性未验证；LLM 编码带来的计算开销与实时性未予讨论；4.4%/5.1% 的相对提升幅度在长期预测中虽显著，但绝对误差水平未给出。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [81] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Halo，一种在已有深度预测器上增加第二输出头（估计分布尺度参数）并在匹配负对数似然下联合训练的方法；实验表明异方差预测不仅用于不确定性量化，还能显著改善点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测（同时估计位置参数与尺度参数）通常被动机化为不确定性量化。然而在时间序列之外的领域，异方差估计曾被报告对点估计产生负面效果，导致该方法的价值被低估。本文旨在澄清并证明：在时间序列预测中，引入尺度估计实际上能够改进点预测（MSE/MAE），从而纠正常见误解并给出可复用的实践方案。

Method: Halo 的核心思想是复用现有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出投影，用于估计其隐式分布的尺度（scale）参数，并在与假设分布匹配的负对数似然（NLL）损失下训练，即同时优化位置与尺度。作者将该改动分别以高斯和拉普拉斯损失形式应用于三类先进模型：Transformer、图网络（结合变分自编码器）、以及单层卷积网络。方法探索了尺度估计的两种实现——额外投影头（second projection head）与完整并行网络（full parallel network）——并比较其影响。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上，Halo 在 30 组「模型-市场-指标」比较中的 28 组提升了 MSE 与 MAE，平均 MSE 降低 2.6%–16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%–11.0%。两项主要结论：(1) 尺度估计来自第二投影头还是完整并行网络影响很小，关键在于网络是否估计尺度；(2) 改进在针对点估计基线已调好的超参数下依然成立，因此重调超参并非必需。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出一个架构无关、易于集成的 Halo 修改，证明异方差估计可作为提升点估计精度的手段，而不仅限于不确定性量化，并在多个模型与市场上验证了稳健的增益。意义在于给出低成本、无需重调超参的改进范式。潜在局限包括：实验集中于电力价格这一时间序列基准，未验证其他时序或跨域任务；仅考虑高斯/拉普拉斯两种分布假设；论文摘要中也未给出不确定性校准质量的系统评估。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [82] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究能否用基于重尾自正则化(heavy-tailed self-regularisation)框架的廉价谱度量作为成员推理攻击(MIA)脆弱性的代理指标，发现stable rank与MIA成功率呈强正相关，Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区域与MIA脆弱性呈稳定负相关，且这些关联比传统泛化差距更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推理攻击(MIA)是审计机器学习模型隐私泄露风险的重要手段，但当前最先进的攻击方法需要训练计算代价高昂的影子模型(shadow models)，导致大规模隐私评估难以实际开展。因此作者希望找到一种无需训练影子模型、计算开销低廉的替代指标来预测MIA脆弱性，从而支持可扩展的隐私审计。同时，作者质疑传统过拟合/泛化差距度量是否足以刻画隐私泄露，并尝试从神经网络谱(spectra)中挖掘隐私相关信息。

Method: 作者采用WeightWatcher工具中的谱度量作为分析对象，这些度量源自重尾自正则化(heavy-tailed self-regularisation)理论框架，可仅通过已训练模型的权重矩阵计算得到，无需训练影子模型。研究在图像分类与表格分类任务上评估多个WeightWatcher谱度量，并将其与MIA隐私泄露程度的相关性，同传统的泛化度量(如泛化差距)进行对比。核心探测量包括stable rank(稳定秩)与Log alpha-Norm。通过相关性分析考察各谱度量与MIA成功率之间的关系，并特别关注低假阳性率(low false-positive regime)下的表现。

Result: 实验覆盖图像与表格两类分类任务。结果显示：跨数据集上，stable rank与整体MIA成功率呈强正相关；Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区域与MIA脆弱性呈一致的负相关。这些关联强度均高于使用泛化差距(generalisation gap)所得到的关联。结果表明神经网络谱中包含未被传统过拟合度量充分捕捉的隐私泄露信息。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了基于重尾自正则化框架的谱度量(尤其是stable rank与Log alpha-Norm)可作为MIA脆弱性的低成本代理指标，其与隐私泄露的关联强于传统泛化度量，为可扩展的隐私审计提供了新方向。意义在于规避了训练影子模型的高昂成本，使大规模隐私评估成为可能。局限方面：本文为相关性分析，并未确立因果机制；评估局限于图像与表格分类任务；谱度量仅为脆弱性的代理而非直接攻击方法，其在不同模型架构、模态与攻击类型下的普适性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [83] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练的设计知识先验，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）把文本提示诱导的生成梯度注入基于密度的拓扑优化的物理迭代循环中，使自然语言成为可被机器解释的工程意图表示，从而生成兼具物理可行性与生物启发式承载模式的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网等自然承载结构以单位质量实现高刚度，但经典拓扑优化器很少收敛到这类几何形态，且难以让工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。该工作旨在解决两个问题：(1) 如何将生物启发的几何先验引入拓扑优化；(2) 如何提供一个可复用、免训练且机器可解释的设计意图接口。这对于轻量化结构设计、增材制造以及需要高效材料分布的工程场景具有重要意义。

Method: 核心思路是把冻结的文本到图像扩散模型当作免训练的"设计知识源"，并通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）将其蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化的物理回路。具体而言：文本提示在每个优化迭代中产生一个生成梯度（prompt-induced generative gradient），该梯度与有限元灵敏度（FEM sensitivity）相结合，由物理决定哪些提示诱导特征得以保留。方法在处理扩散-物理耦合时出现明显的中间密度倾向，使用带 β-continuation 的 Heaviside 投影加以解决。此外，作者构建了一个自动化的骨架（skeleton）提取流程，将优化得到的密度场转换为可用于 CAD 的候选几何。框架通过更换文本提示即可跨域、跨载荷条件、跨物理目标重新定向生成先验，而每个新问题的物理设置单独指定。

Result: 实验在四个几何域、两种物理机制下进行了 245 次主要 SDS 运行。在 49 个提示-域组合中，有 38 个实现统计显著的柔度下降（力学最高 −31.5%，热弹性最高 −23.0%），优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形态学分析识别出反复出现的改进结构特征：在多数域中，生成先验抑制了肋骨架中的死端分支（dead-end branches），端点-柔度相关性达 r = +0.56 至 +0.99。Heaviside 投影配合 β-continuation 将中间密度倾向从 42.6% 降至 3% 以下；基于骨架的自动化流程可将优化密度场转换为面向 CAD 的候选几何。

Conclusion: 该工作表明预训练生成模型可作为免训练、可复用的工程结构设计先验，将自然语言转化为机器可解释的设计意图表示，并与物理仿真紧密耦合以筛选有效特征。主要贡献包括：SDS 与拓扑优化物理回路的耦合机制、跨域跨物理情形的系统性验证、以及中间密度问题的 Heaviside β-continuation 解决方案和面向 CAD 的骨架化后处理。潜在局限：评估仍以柔度（力学与热弹性）为主要指标；生成先验的引入带来额外计算开销；跨域迁移依赖精心设计的提示；方法有效性未在更复杂的多物理或制造约束下充分验证。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [84] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将联邦学习（FL）应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端以及单一固定聚合服务器的单点信任与故障问题，提出了一套组合解决方案：构建室内火灾检测数据集、可边缘部署的轻量检测器（模型更新压缩高达10倍），以及一种半去中心化的拜占庭鲁棒FL方法（历史感知聚合 + 轮换协调者）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统通常依赖边缘摄像头，其录制的敏感视频数据难以集中收集至中央服务器，因此联邦学习成为自然的隐私保护选择。然而现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍未被解决：(1) 上行带宽有限，使频繁传输大规模模型更新不切实际；(2) 存在拜占庭客户端（恶意或故障节点），会污染全局模型；(3) 对单一且永久固定的聚合服务器无条件信任，形成单点故障与信任假设过强的问题。该研究旨在同时解决这三方面问题，使火灾检测FL系统在真实边缘与分布式环境下可落地。

Method: 论文提出三项技术贡献：(i) 数据层面，从八个公开来源整理构建了一个室内火灾检测数据集，用于训练与评估；(ii) 模型层面，设计了一个可边缘部署的轻量检测器，对模型更新进行压缩，压缩比最高达10倍，仅带来较小的平衡准确率损失，以缓解上行带宽瓶颈；(iii) 算法层面，提出一种半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，将“历史感知聚合”（history-aware aggregation，利用历史信息识别持续性异常更新）与“轮换协调者”（rotating coordinator，避免固定服务器）相结合。该设计可驱逐那些单轮过滤机制难以发现的隐蔽（stealthy）攻击，同时消除固定服务器带来的单点故障。

Result: 在留出的测试集上，采用轮换协调者的方法与固定服务器版本在准确率和检测速度上表现相当，表明去中心化协调未造成性能退化。此外，作者通过一个物理分布的六节点云部署验证了系统的实际可行性。评估指标涉及平衡准确率（balanced accuracy）与检测速度，并考察了模型更新压缩（最高10倍）对精度的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于将数据集构建、边缘高效模型更新压缩与拜占庭鲁棒半去中心化聚合三者结合，针对室内火灾检测这一隐私敏感且资源受限的边缘场景，同时缓解了带宽、恶意客户端与单点信任三重问题，并通过六节点物理部署证明了工程可行性。潜在局限包括：摘要未披露数据集规模、客户端数量、攻击类型与强度、压缩带来的精度下降具体数值，以及轮换协调机制在大规模或高频轮换下的通信与同步开销评估。论文未涉及事件相机或脉冲神经网络等神经形态感知方向。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [85] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇综述性论文，系统梳理了2015年至今AI与深度学习（尤其是CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强）在肺癌放射学影像检测中的应用现状，同时指出临床落地所面临的标准化数据、模型可解释性、隐私与伦理等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后与生存质量至关重要。然而医学影像判读过程复杂，高度依赖经过训练的专业放射科医生，存在人力不足与主观差异等问题。AI与深度学习具备自动化、优化影像分析流程的潜力，因此有必要对近年相关研究进行系统综述，明确技术现状、有效方法及尚待解决的瓶颈，从而为临床安全应用提供参考。

Method: 本文属于文献综述而非提出新模型。作者在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行系统性检索，筛选出2015年至今发表的96篇涉及AI与DL在生物医学工程中应用的文献。分析重点聚焦于卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习（Transfer Learning）与数据增强（Data Augmentation）的技术路线，讨论其在提升影像判读准确率与效率方面的作用，并归纳当前方法学与临床应用层面的局限。

Result: 综述结果表明，AI与DL方法在肺癌早期诊断中可作为有效辅助手段，报告了较高的敏感度（sensitivity）与特异度（specificity）。CNN配合迁移学习和数据增强被视为提升影像分析精度与效率的关键技术。同时文献也识别出若干制约因素：缺乏标准化数据集、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私保护，以及伦理与社会层面的影响等。

Conclusion: 该综述的主要贡献在于对近年AI/DL用于肺癌放射学检测的研究进行了系统整理，确认其在改善肺癌患者诊疗方面的积极潜力，并强调迁移学习与数据增强等技术的实用价值。其局限在于：作为综述未提出新的算法或实验验证，且明确指出该领域仍需更多研究以及相应监管规范，才能保证临床应用的质量与可靠性。此外，本文内容与事件相机、脉冲神经网络、UAV感知或实时边缘AI等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [86] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG
